*Yewen Li,Zhiyi Lyu,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Fei Pan,Bo An,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出分层大语言模型LBM用于自动竞价，包含高层推理模型LBM-Think和低层动作生成模型LBM-Act，通过双嵌入机制融合语言与数值输入，使用GQPO离线强化微调技术减少幻觉，提升竞价性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告平台竞价规模扩大使手动竞价不切实际，现有自动竞价方法存在黑盒训练、数据集覆盖有限、难以理解任务状态和泛化能力不足等问题。大语言模型具有先验知识和推理能力，但直接应用于自动竞价面临精确动作生成困难和专业知识缺乏的挑战。

Method: 提出分层大自动竞价模型LBM：1) LBM-Think高层推理模型；2) LBM-Act低层动作生成模型。采用双嵌入机制融合语言和数值模态进行语言引导训练；提出GQPO离线强化微调技术，无需仿真或实际部署即可减少LLM幻觉并提升决策性能。

Result: 实验证明基于LBM的生成式骨干模型具有优越性，特别是在高效训练方式和泛化能力方面表现突出。

Conclusion: LBM通过分层架构有效结合大语言模型的推理能力和专业竞价知识，解决了自动竞价中的黑盒问题和泛化挑战，为动态广告环境中的智能竞价提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The growing scale of ad auctions on online advertising platforms has intensified competition, making manual bidding impractical and necessitating auto-bidding to help advertisers achieve their economic goals. Current auto-bidding methods have evolved to use offline reinforcement learning or generative methods to optimize bidding strategies, but they can sometimes behave counterintuitively due to the black-box training manner and limited mode coverage of datasets, leading to challenges in understanding task status and generalization in dynamic ad environments. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising solution by leveraging prior human knowledge and reasoning abilities to improve auto-bidding performance. However, directly applying LLMs to auto-bidding faces difficulties due to the need for precise actions in competitive auctions and the lack of specialized auto-bidding knowledge, which can lead to hallucinations and suboptimal decisions. To address these challenges, we propose a hierarchical Large autoBidding Model (LBM) to leverage the reasoning capabilities of LLMs for developing a superior auto-bidding strategy. This includes a high-level LBM-Think model for reasoning and a low-level LBM-Act model for action generation. Specifically, we propose a dual embedding mechanism to efficiently fuse two modalities, including language and numerical inputs, for language-guided training of the LBM-Act; then, we propose an offline reinforcement fine-tuning technique termed GQPO for mitigating the LLM-Think's hallucinations and enhancing decision-making performance without simulation or real-world rollout like previous multi-turn LLM-based methods. Experiments demonstrate the superiority of a generative backbone based on our LBM, especially in an efficient training manner and generalization ability.

</details>


### [115] [Representation Fidelity:Auditing Algorithmic Decisions About Humans Using Self-Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05136)
*Theresa Elstner,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"表征保真度"作为验证算法决策的新维度，通过比较外部规定的输入表征与人类自我描述之间的差异来评估决策的合理性，并基于德国信用数据集构建了首个包含3万条合成自然语言自我描述的贷款决策基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法决策验证主要关注公平性、准确性和透明度，但缺乏对决策所基于的表征是否合理的基础性评估。需要一种新方法来衡量算法决策是否建立在合理的表征基础上，即决策是否基于对个体的准确理解。

Method: 提出表征保真度概念，通过比较两种表征的差异来操作化：1)算法决策所基于的外部规定输入表征；2)决策对象提供的自我描述（仅用于验证）。分析表征间差异的性质，开发量化方法，建立表征不匹配的通用类型学，并基于德国信用数据集构建包含3万条合成自然语言自我描述的贷款决策基准数据集。

Result: 建立了表征保真度的概念框架和操作化方法，开发了表征不匹配的类型学，并创建了首个用于评估表征保真度的基准数据集——Loan-Granting Self-Representations Corpus 2025，包含3万条合成自然语言自我描述及专家标注的表征不匹配信息。

Conclusion: 表征保真度为算法决策验证提供了新的重要维度，能够评估决策是否基于合理的表征基础。该方法通过比较外部表征与自我描述的差异，为理解算法决策的合理性提供了系统框架，所构建的基准数据集将推动该领域的研究发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new dimension for validating algorithmic decisions about humans by measuring the fidelity of their representations. Representation Fidelity measures if decisions about a person rest on reasonable grounds. We propose to operationalize this notion by measuring the distance between two representations of the same person: (1) an externally prescribed input representation on which the decision is based, and (2) a self-description provided by the human subject of the decision, used solely to validate the input representation. We examine the nature of discrepancies between these representations, how such discrepancies can be quantified, and derive a generic typology of representation mismatches that determine the degree of representation fidelity. We further present the first benchmark for evaluating representation fidelity based on a dataset of loan-granting decisions. Our Loan-Granting Self-Representations Corpus 2025 consists of a large corpus of 30 000 synthetic natural language self-descriptions derived from corresponding representations of applicants in the German Credit Dataset, along with expert annotations of representation mismatches between each pair of representations.

</details>


### [116] [Feature Resemblance: On the Theoretical Understanding of Analogical Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05143)
*Ruichen Xu,Wenjing Yan,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文证明Transformer通过联合训练相似性和属性前提实现类比推理，需要特定训练顺序，且两跳推理可简化为带恒等桥的类比推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估混淆多种推理类型，需要隔离类比推理（基于已知相似性推断实体间共享属性）并分析其在Transformer中的涌现机制

Method: 理论证明三个关键结果：1)联合训练相似性和属性前提通过表征对齐实现类比推理；2)顺序训练需要先学相似性结构再学具体属性；3)两跳推理可简化为带恒等桥的类比推理。实验验证使用高达15亿参数的架构

Result: 实验验证了理论预测，表明Transformer将具有相似属性的实体编码为相似表征，通过特征对齐实现属性转移，表征几何形状塑造了归纳推理能力

Conclusion: Transformer通过统一机制实现类比推理：将相似实体编码为相似表征，通过特征对齐实现属性转移。训练顺序和数据中显式恒等桥对成功推理至关重要

Abstract: Understanding reasoning in large language models is complicated by evaluations that conflate multiple reasoning types. We isolate analogical reasoning (inferring shared properties between entities based on known similarities) and analyze its emergence in transformers. We theoretically prove three key results: (1) Joint training on similarity and attribution premises enables analogical reasoning through aligned representations; (2) Sequential training succeeds only when similarity structure is learned before specific attributes, revealing a necessary curriculum; (3) Two-hop reasoning ($a \to b, b \to c \implies a \to c$) reduces to analogical reasoning with identity bridges ($b = b$), which must appear explicitly in training data. These results reveal a unified mechanism: transformers encode entities with similar properties into similar representations, enabling property transfer through feature alignment. Experiments with architectures up to 1.5B parameters validate our theory and demonstrate how representational geometry shapes inductive reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [C2-Faith: Benchmarking LLM Judges for Causal and Coverage Faithfulness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05167)
*Avni Mittal,Rauno Arike*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: C2-Faith基准评估LLM作为思维链推理评判者的可靠性，重点关注因果性和覆盖度两个忠实度维度，发现模型表现高度依赖任务框架且存在检测与定位之间的显著差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型越来越多地被用作思维链推理的评判者，但尚不清楚它们能否可靠地评估过程忠实度（而不仅仅是答案合理性）。需要系统评估LLM在因果性和覆盖度这两个互补维度上的判断能力

Method: 从PRM800K构建C2-Faith基准，通过受控扰动创建已知因果错误位置的示例（用非因果变体替换单个步骤），以及不同删除率的受控覆盖度删除。评估三个前沿评判模型在三个任务上的表现：二元因果检测、因果步骤定位和覆盖度评分

Result: 模型排名强烈依赖于任务框架，没有单一评判者在所有设置中占主导；所有评判者在检测错误和定位错误之间存在显著差距；对于不完整推理，覆盖度判断存在系统性高估

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了LLM评判者在何时可靠、在何处失败，为过程级评估中选择评判者提供了实用指导，强调了评估框架对结果的重要影响

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but it remains unclear whether they can reliably assess process faithfulness rather than just answer plausibility. We introduce C2-Faith, a benchmark built from PRM800K that targets two complementary dimensions of faithfulness: causality (does each step logically follow from prior context?) and coverage (are essential intermediate inferences present?). Using controlled perturbations, we create examples with known causal error positions by replacing a single step with an acausal variant, and with controlled coverage deletions at varying deletion rates (scored against reference labels). We evaluate three frontier judges under three tasks: binary causal detection, causal step localization, and coverage scoring. The results show that model rankings depend strongly on task framing, with no single judge dominating all settings; all judges exhibit a substantial gap between detecting an error and localizing it; and coverage judgments are systematically inflated for incomplete reasoning. These findings clarify when LLM judges are dependable and where they fail, and provide practical guidance for selecting judges in process-level evaluation

</details>


### [118] [Sparse-BitNet: 1.58-bit LLMs are Naturally Friendly to Semi-Structured Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05168)
*Di Zhang,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Yudong Wang,Hanyong Shao,Yingbo Hao,Zewen Chi,Li Dong,Ting Song,Yan Xia,Zhifang Sui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Sparse-BitNet：首个将1.58位BitNet量化与动态N:M稀疏化统一结合的框架，在多种模型规模和训练机制下，相比全精度模型在相同稀疏度下性能下降更小，并能容忍更高的结构化稀疏度，同时通过定制稀疏张量核心实现训练和推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化N:M稀疏和低位量化（如1.58位BitNet）是提升大语言模型效率的两个有前景的方法，但以往研究多孤立进行。本文旨在探究这两种技术的相互作用，发现1.58位BitNet天然比全精度模型更兼容N:M稀疏，从而提出统一框架来联合应用这两种技术。

Method: 提出Sparse-BitNet框架，首次联合应用1.58位量化和动态N:M稀疏化，并确保训练稳定性。框架包含定制稀疏张量核心，支持多种模型规模和训练机制（稀疏预训练和稠密到稀疏调度）。

Result: 在相同稀疏度下，1.58位BitNet相比全精度基线性能下降更小，且能容忍更高的结构化稀疏度才出现精度崩溃。通过定制稀疏张量核心，Sparse-BitNet在训练和推理中实现高达1.30倍的加速。

Conclusion: 将极低位量化与半结构化N:M稀疏结合是大语言模型效率优化的有前景方向。Sparse-BitNet展示了这种组合的优越性，为高效LLM提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Semi-structured N:M sparsity and low-bit quantization (e.g., 1.58-bit BitNet) are two promising approaches for improving the efficiency of large language models (LLMs), yet they have largely been studied in isolation. In this work, we investigate their interaction and show that 1.58-bit BitNet is naturally more compatible with N:M sparsity than full-precision models. To study this effect, we propose Sparse-BitNet, a unified framework that jointly applies 1.58-bit quantization and dynamic N:M sparsification while ensuring stable training for the first time. Across multiple model scales and training regimes (sparse pretraining and dense-to-sparse schedules), 1.58-bit BitNet consistently exhibits smaller performance degradation than full-precision baselines at the same sparsity levels and can tolerate higher structured sparsity before accuracy collapse. Moreover, using our custom sparse tensor core, Sparse-BitNet achieves substantial speedups in both training and inference, reaching up to 1.30X. These results highlight that combining extremely low-bit quantization with semi-structured N:M sparsity is a promising direction for efficient LLMs. Code available at https://github.com/AAzdi/Sparse-BitNet

</details>


### [119] [Guidelines for the Annotation and Visualization of Legal Argumentation Structures in Chinese Judicial Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05171)
*Kun Chen,Xianglei Liao,Kaixue Fei,Yi Xing,Xinrui Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出用于司法判决中法律论证结构标注的系统化操作框架，包含命题分类、关系定义、形式化表示规则和标准化标注流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示司法推理的逻辑组织，为计算分析提供可靠数据基础，支持大规模司法推理分析及法律论证挖掘、计算建模和AI辅助法律分析等研究。

Method: 建立包含命题层面（四种命题类型：一般规范性、特定规范性、一般事实性、特定事实性）和关系层面（五种关系：支持、攻击、联合、匹配、同一性）的标注框架，制定形式化表示规则、可视化规范和标准化标注流程。

Result: 开发出系统化的法律论证结构标注指南，包含清晰的概念模型、形式化表示规则和实用标注程序，支持复杂论证模式的一致图形表示。

Conclusion: 该指南为司法推理的大规模分析以及法律论证挖掘、法律推理计算建模和AI辅助法律分析等未来研究提供了方法论支持。

Abstract: This guideline proposes a systematic and operational annotation framework for representing the structure of legal argumentation in judicial decisions. Grounded in theories of legal reasoning and argumentation, the framework aims to reveal the logical organization of judicial reasoning and to provide a reliable data foundation for computational analysis. At the proposition level, the guideline distinguishes four types of propositions: general normative propositions, specific normative propositions, general factual propositions, and specific factual propositions. At the relational level, five types of relations are defined to capture argumentative structures: support, attack, joint, match, and identity. These relations represent positive and negative argumentative connections, conjunctive reasoning structures, the correspondence between legal norms and case facts, and semantic equivalence between propositions. The guideline further specifies formal representation rules and visualization conventions for both basic and nested structures, enabling consistent graphical representation of complex argumentation patterns. In addition, it establishes a standardized annotation workflow and consistency control mechanisms to ensure reproducibility and reliability of the annotated data. By providing a clear conceptual model, formal representation rules, and practical annotation procedures, this guideline offers methodological support for large-scale analysis of judicial reasoning and for future research in legal argument mining, computational modeling of legal reasoning, and AI-assisted legal analysis.

</details>


### [120] [Transducing Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05193)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Tianyu Liu,Reda Boumasmoud,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于有限状态转换器的语言模型转换框架，通过确定性字符串转换将预训练语言模型适配到特定输出格式，无需修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型定义在字符串上的分布，但下游任务常需要不同输出格式（如字节级到词级、DNA到氨基酸序列）。现有方法未将确定性字符串转换视为产生新功能语言模型的通用框架。

Method: 引入基于有限状态转换器的语言模型转换框架，开发算法将语言模型与FST组合，通过边缘化源字符串映射到目标字符串，在转换器中传播概率而不修改模型参数，支持对转换输出的条件化。提供精确算法、高效近似和理论分析。

Result: 在三个领域进行实验：将语言模型从token转换为字节、从token转换为词、从DNA转换为氨基酸。实验表明该方法能在推理时适配预训练语言模型以满足应用特定的输出要求。

Conclusion: 确定性字符串转换可产生新的功能完整的语言模型，有限状态转换器为此提供了有效框架，实现了推理时对预训练语言模型的输出格式适配，无需重新训练。

Abstract: Modern language models define distributions over strings, but downstream tasks often require different output formats. For instance, a model that generates byte-pair strings does not directly produce word-level predictions, and a DNA model does not directly produce amino-acid sequences. In such cases, a deterministic string-to-string transformation can convert the model's output to the desired form. This is a familiar pattern in probability theory: applying a function $f$ to a random variable $X\sim p$ yields a transformed random variable $f(X)$ with an induced distribution. While such transformations are occasionally used in language modeling, prior work does not treat them as yielding new, fully functional language models. We formalize this perspective and introduce a general framework for language models derived from deterministic string-to-string transformations. We focus on transformations representable as finite-state transducers -- a commonly used state-machine abstraction for efficient string-to-string mappings. We develop algorithms that compose a language model with an FST to *marginalize* over source strings mapping to a given target, propagating probabilities through the transducer without altering model parameters and enabling *conditioning* on transformed outputs. We present an exact algorithm, an efficient approximation, and a theoretical analysis. We conduct experiments in three domains: converting language models from tokens to bytes, from tokens to words, and from DNA to amino acids. These experiments demonstrate inference-time adaptation of pretrained language models to match application-specific output requirements.

</details>


### [121] [Diffusion LLMs can think EoS-by-EoS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05197)
*Sarah Breckner,Sebastian Schuster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 扩散LLMs通过EoS（序列结束）标记作为隐藏计算空间，提升复杂推理能力，特别是在生成长度远超实际答案所需时表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现扩散语言模型在复杂推理任务上表现优于自回归模型，特别是当生成长度远超所需答案长度时，模型通过EoS标记填充答案。作者假设EoS标记作为隐藏计算空间，使模型能够解决更难的推理问题。

Method: 使用LLaDA1.5、LLaDA2.0-mini和Dream-v0扩散模型，在加法、实体追踪和数独任务上进行实验。通过控制提示实验验证EoS标记的作用，并通过因果干预（将EoS标记的隐藏状态替换为反事实生成的隐藏状态）来验证其作为计算空间的功能。

Result: 添加EoS标记显著提升模型的推理能力；因果干预实验显示，替换EoS标记的隐藏状态会频繁改变生成输出为反事实结果，证实EoS标记携带解决问题的信息。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型确实通过EoS标记进行"逐EoS思考"，这些看似无意义的标记实际上作为隐藏计算空间，承载解决问题的信息，从而提升复杂推理能力。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs have been proposed as an alternative to autoregressive LLMs, excelling especially at complex reasoning tasks with interdependent sub-goals. Curiously, this is particularly true if the generation length, i.e., the number of tokens the model has to output, is set to a much higher value than is required for providing the correct answer to the task, and the model pads its answer with end-of-sequence (EoS) tokens. We hypothesize that diffusion models think EoS-by-EoS, that is, they use the representations of EoS tokens as a hidden scratchpad, which allows them to solve harder reasoning problems. We experiment with the diffusion models LLaDA1.5, LLaDA2.0-mini, and Dream-v0 on the tasks Addition, Entity Tracking, and Sudoku. In a controlled prompting experiment, we confirm that adding EoS tokens improves the LLMs' reasoning capabilities. To further verify whether they serve as space for hidden computations, we patch the hidden states of the EoS tokens with those of a counterfactual generation, which frequently changes the generated output to the counterfactual. The success of the causal intervention underscores that the EoS tokens, which one may expect to be devoid of meaning, carry information on the problem to solve. The behavioral experiments and the causal interventions indicate that diffusion LLMs can indeed think EoS-by-EoS.

</details>


### [122] [Distilling Formal Logic into Neural Spaces: A Kernel Alignment Approach for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05198)
*Sara Candussio,Gabriele Sarti,Gaia Saveri,Luca Bortolussi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过蒸馏语义几何学习形式化规范连续神经表示的框架，将符号鲁棒性核蒸馏到Transformer编码器中，实现高效、可扩展的神经符号推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：符号核方法计算成本高、锚点依赖且不可逆；基于语法的神经嵌入无法捕捉底层语义结构。需要一种能兼顾语义保持和计算效率的方法

Method: 采用师生设置，将符号鲁棒性核蒸馏到Transformer编码器中。使用连续的、核加权的几何对齐目标进行监督，根据语义差异比例惩罚错误。训练后编码器单次前向传播即可生成嵌入

Result: 在信号时序逻辑（STL）上验证，神经表示能忠实保持STL公式的语义相似性，准确预测鲁棒性和约束满足性，保持内在可逆性，计算成本远低于原始核方法

Conclusion: 该框架实现了高效、可扩展的神经符号推理和公式重建，无需运行时重复核计算，为形式化规范的神经表示学习提供了新途径

Abstract: We introduce a framework for learning continuous neural representations of formal specifications by distilling the geometry of their semantics into a latent space. Existing approaches rely either on symbolic kernels -- which preserve behavioural semantics but are computationally prohibitive, anchor-dependent, and non-invertible -- or on syntax-based neural embeddings that fail to capture underlying structures. Our method bridges this gap: using a teacher-student setup, we distill a symbolic robustness kernel into a Transformer encoder. Unlike standard contrastive methods, we supervise the model with a continuous, kernel-weighted geometric alignment objective that penalizes errors in proportion to their semantic discrepancies. Once trained, the encoder produces embeddings in a single forward pass, effectively mimicking the kernel's logic at a fraction of its computational cost. We apply our framework to Signal Temporal Logic (STL), demonstrating that the resulting neural representations faithfully preserve the semantic similarity of STL formulae, accurately predict robustness and constraint satisfaction, and remain intrinsically invertible. Our proposed approach enables highly efficient, scalable neuro-symbolic reasoning and formula reconstruction without repeated kernel computation at runtime.

</details>


### [123] [Balancing Coverage and Draft Latency in Vocabulary Trimming for Faster Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05210)
*Ofir Ben Shoham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过词汇表裁剪优化推测解码中的草稿模型，在保持高覆盖率的同时显著降低延迟，提升推理吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中草稿模型的词汇表大小存在根本性权衡：大词汇表提高token覆盖率和与目标模型的一致性，但增加延迟；小词汇表降低延迟但可能遗漏关键token。针对领域特定工作负载只使用完整词汇表的一小部分这一观察，提出词汇表裁剪方法

Method: 将草稿模型词汇表选择建模为约束优化问题，平衡token覆盖率和草稿延迟。覆盖率基于训练数据中的助手响应计算，延迟使用架构感知的FLOPs估计（捕捉语言建模头成本与词汇表大小的函数关系）。使用树结构Parzen估计器优化效用函数，在最小覆盖率约束下探索覆盖率-延迟Pareto前沿

Result: 实验显示：草稿词汇表最多减少97%同时保持高覆盖率；在领域特定任务上实现最多16%延迟降低和20%吞吐量提升；在多样化分布外任务上获得最多6.7%吞吐量增益

Conclusion: 词汇表裁剪是优化推测解码草稿模型的有效方法，通过选择性减少词汇表大小，在保持高覆盖率的同时显著降低延迟，提升整体推理效率

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates inference for Large Language Models by using a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by a larger target model. Prior work shows that the draft model often dominates speculative decoding latency, since it generates tokens sequentially and incurs high cost from its language modeling head as vocabulary size grows. This exposes a fundamental trade-off in draft model design: larger vocabularies improve token coverage and agreement with the target model, but incur higher draft latency, while smaller vocabularies reduce latency at the risk of missing tokens required for accurate draft generation. We address this trade-off through vocabulary trimming for draft models, motivated by the observation that domain-specific workloads use only a small fraction of the full vocabulary. We cast draft vocabulary selection as a constrained optimization problem that balances token coverage and draft latency. Coverage is computed over assistant responses in the training data, while latency is estimated using architecture-aware FLOPs that capture the cost of the language modeling head as a function of vocabulary size. We optimize a utility function with a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator to efficiently explore the coverage-latency Pareto frontier under a minimum coverage constraint. Experiments show improved speculative decoding throughput while reducing draft vocabularies by up to 97% with high coverage. On domain-specific tasks, we achieve up to 16% latency reduction and 20% throughput improvement, and up to 6.7% throughput gains on diverse out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [124] [A theoretical model of dynamical grammatical gender shifting based on set-valued set function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03510)
*Mohamed El Idrissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于模板的模块化认知模型，用于解释名词的语义和形态句法特征变化，特别是语法性别转换现象，通过数学形式化方法预测词汇项到形态模板的非线性动态映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索名词的多样性特征，包括语义（可数/不可数）和形态句法（阳性/阴性）区分，特别关注语法性别转换这一跨语言普遍现象，旨在通过形式化模型揭示词汇变异的底层模式。

Method: 提出了一种新的计算组件——基于模板的模块化认知模型，该模型通过集合值集合函数 h: P(M) → P(M) 形式化表示词汇项与形态模板的配对过程。模型预测词汇项到形态模板的非线性动态映射，并通过里夫语（Riffian）的实证观察验证。

Result: 研究结果表明，词汇变化中出现的性别转换和非性别转换源于模板转换过程。通过形式化证明转换机制适用于名词到名词的派生，挑战并扩展了传统的构词观点。该模型为跨语言形态标记的复杂性提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 该数学模型不仅深化了对形态句法变异的理解，还为需要精确建模语言模式的其他领域提供了潜在应用。研究通过形式化方法揭示了词汇项与形态模板配对的底层机制，为语言变异研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This study investigates the diverse characteristics of nouns, focusing on both semantic (e.g., countable/uncountable) and morphosyntactic (e.g., masculine/feminine) distinctions. We explore inter-word variations for gender markers in noun morphology. Grammatical gender shift is a widespread phenomenon in languages around the world. The aim is to uncover through a formal model the underlying patterns governing the variation of lexemes. To this end, we propose a new computational component dedicated to pairing items with morphological templates (e.g., the result of a generated item-template pair: (funas, $\{N, +SG, -PL, -M, +F, -COL, +SING\}$), with its spell-out form: $ð$a-funast 'cow'). This process is formally represented by the Template-Based and Modular Cognitive model. This proposed model, defined by a set-valued set function $h : \mathscr{P}(M) \rightarrow \mathscr{P}(M)$, predicts the nonlinear dynamic mapping of lexical items onto morphological templates. By applying this formalism, we present a unified framework for understanding the complexities of morphological markings across languages. Through empirical observations, we demonstrate how these shifts, as well as non-gender shifts, arise during lexical changes, especially in Riffian. Our model posits that these variant markings emerge due to template shifts occurring during word and meaning's formation. By formally demonstrating that conversion is applicable to noun-to-noun derivation, we challenge and broaden the conventional view of word formation. This mathematical model not only contributes to a deeper understanding of morphosyntactic variation but also offers potential applications in other fields requiring precise modelling of linguistic patterns.

</details>


### [125] [Med-V1: Small Language Models for Zero-shot and Scalable Biomedical Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05308)
*Qiao Jin,Yin Fang,Lauren He,Yifan Yang,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhizheng Wang,Nicholas Wan,Joey Chan,Donald C. Comeau,Robert Leaman,Charalampos S. Floudas,Aidong Zhang,Michael F. Chiang,Yifan Peng,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Med-V1是一个仅30亿参数的小型语言模型，专门用于生物医学证据归因和验证任务，性能媲美前沿大模型但部署成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型（如GPT-5）在生物医学证据归因任务中表现优异，但部署成本过高，难以大规模应用。需要开发高效、准确且轻量化的替代方案。

Method: 开发了Med-V1系列小型语言模型（仅30亿参数），使用本研究新开发的高质量合成数据进行训练。将五个生物医学基准统一为验证格式进行评估。

Result: Med-V1在五个生物医学基准上比基础模型提升27.0%至71.3%，性能与GPT-5等前沿大模型相当，并能提供高质量的解释。应用研究发现：引用格式指令强烈影响引用有效性和幻觉率；GPT-5生成更多声明但幻觉率与GPT-4o相似；Med-V1能自动识别临床实践指南中的高风险证据误归因。

Conclusion: Med-V1为生物医学证据归因和验证任务提供了一个高效、准确的轻量化替代方案，性能媲美前沿大模型但部署成本更低，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Assessing whether an article supports an assertion is essential for hallucination detection and claim verification. While large language models (LLMs) have the potential to automate this task, achieving strong performance requires frontier models such as GPT-5 that are prohibitively expensive to deploy at scale. To efficiently perform biomedical evidence attribution, we present Med-V1, a family of small language models with only three billion parameters. Trained on high-quality synthetic data newly developed in this study, Med-V1 substantially outperforms (+27.0% to +71.3%) its base models on five biomedical benchmarks unified into a verification format. Despite its smaller size, Med-V1 performs comparably to frontier LLMs such as GPT-5, along with high-quality explanations for its predictions. We use Med-V1 to conduct a first-of-its-kind use case study that quantifies hallucinations in LLM-generated answers under different citation instructions. Results show that the format instruction strongly affects citation validity and hallucination, with GPT-5 generating more claims but exhibiting hallucination rates similar to GPT-4o. Additionally, we present a second use case showing that Med-V1 can automatically identify high-stakes evidence misattributions in clinical practice guidelines, revealing potentially negative public health impacts that are otherwise challenging to identify at scale. Overall, Med-V1 provides an efficient and accurate lightweight alternative to frontier LLMs for practical and real-world applications in biomedical evidence attribution and verification tasks. Med-V1 is available at https://github.com/ncbi-nlp/Med-V1.

</details>


### [126] [PersianPunc: A Large-Scale Dataset and BERT-Based Approach for Persian Punctuation Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05314)
*Mohammad Javad Ranjbar Kalahroodi,Heshaam Faili,Azadeh Shakery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了PersianPunc数据集和基于ParsBERT的波斯语标点恢复模型，解决了波斯语标点恢复任务中数据稀缺的问题，并展示了BERT模型相比大语言模型在效率和准确性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语标点恢复对于提升自动语音识别输出的可读性和下游应用价值至关重要，但该领域研究不足且缺乏高质量数据集。现有方法存在过校正倾向和计算成本高的问题。

Method: 通过系统聚合和过滤现有文本资源构建了包含1700万样本的PersianPunc数据集；将标点恢复定义为词元级序列标注任务；对ParsBERT进行微调；与大型语言模型进行对比评估。

Result: 基于BERT的轻量级方法在测试集上取得了91.33%的宏平均F1分数，同时保持实时应用所需的效率。大型语言模型存在过校正倾向（在标点插入之外引入不必要编辑）和计算需求显著更高的问题。

Conclusion: 提出的PersianPunc数据集和基于ParsBERT的模型为波斯语NLP研究提供了重要资源，其轻量级框架适用于形态丰富、资源匮乏的语言，且相比大型语言模型在效率和准确性上更具优势。

Abstract: Punctuation restoration is essential for improving the readability and downstream utility of automatic speech recognition (ASR) outputs, yet remains underexplored for Persian despite its importance. We introduce PersianPunc, a large-scale, high-quality dataset of 17 million samples for Persian punctuation restoration, constructed through systematic aggregation and filtering of existing textual resources. We formulate punctuation restoration as a token-level sequence labeling task and fine-tune ParsBERT to achieve strong performance. Through comparative evaluation, we demonstrate that while large language models can perform punctuation restoration, they suffer from critical limitations: over-correction tendencies that introduce undesired edits beyond punctuation insertion (particularly problematic for speech-to-text pipelines) and substantially higher computational requirements. Our lightweight BERT-based approach achieves a macro-averaged F1 score of 91.33% on our test set while maintaining efficiency suitable for real-time applications. We make our dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MohammadJRanjbar/persian-punctuation-restoration) and model (https://huggingface.co/MohammadJRanjbar/parsbert-persian-punctuation) publicly available to facilitate future research in Persian NLP and provide a scalable framework applicable to other morphologically rich, low-resource languages.

</details>


### [127] [A Multilingual Human Annotated Corpus of Original and Easy-to-Read Texts to Support Access to Democratic Participatory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05345)
*Stefan Bott,Verena Riegler,Horacio Saggion,Almudena Rascón Alcaina,Nouran Khallaf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个包含西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语的高质量人工简化文本语料库，用于支持自动文本简化研究，特别是针对民主参与领域的易读语言应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本简化研究常受限于高质量训练和评估材料的可用性，对于英语以外的语言（如西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语、意大利语）这一问题更为突出。需要为这些资源较少的语言提供高质量的人工简化语料库，以支持易读语言在民主参与中的应用研究。

Method: 在iDEM项目框架下，从民主参与相关领域收集原始文本，基于相关性、版权可用性和伦理标准选择不同文本类型。所有文本由文本简化专家人工简化至易读语言水平，特别创建了首个加泰罗尼亚语标注语料库。

Result: 创建了一个包含西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语的高质量人工简化文本语料库，包含民主参与相关领域的多种文本类型。该语料库特别有价值的是包含了首个加泰罗尼亚语标注语料库，并为西班牙语和意大利语提供了罕见的高质量人工标注语言资源。

Conclusion: 该语料库填补了西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语在高质量人工简化文本资源方面的空白，将免费向公众开放，支持自动文本简化研究，特别是易读语言在民主参与中的应用评估。

Abstract: Being able to understand information is a key factor for a self-determined life and society. It is also very important for participating in democratic processes. The study of automatic text simplification is often limited by the availability of high quality material for the training and evaluation on automatic simplifiers. This is true for English, but more so for less resourced languages like Spanish, Catalan and Italian. In order to fill this gap, we present a corpus of original texts for these 3 languages, with high quality simplification produced by human experts in text simplification. It was developed within the iDEM project to assess the impact of Easy-to-Read (E2R) language for democratic participation. The original texts were compiled from domains related to this topic. The corpus includes different text types, selected based on relevance, copyright availability, and ethical standards. All texts were simplified to E2R level. The corpus is particularity valuable because it includes the first annotated corpus of its kind for the Catalan language. It also represents a noteworthy contribution for Spanish and Italian, offering high-quality, human-annotated language resources that are rarely available in these domains. The corpus will be made freely accessible to the public.

</details>


### [128] [Exploring the potential and limitations of Model Merging for Multi-Domain Adaptation in ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05354)
*Carlos Carvalho,Francisco Teixeira,Thomas Rolland,Alberto Abad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多领域ASR的模型合并技术，提出了基于TSV-M改进的BoostedTSV-M算法，在10个欧洲葡萄牙语领域上评估了11种合并算法，性能优于全微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语音基础模型通常通过领域特定微调进行适配，产生多个定制化检查点。当新数据可用时，重复全微调计算成本过高。模型合并作为多任务训练的可扩展替代方案，可将多个专业模型能力整合到单一模型中。

Method: 研究了多领域ASR的模型合并，提出了BoostedTSV-M算法（基于TSV-M改进），通过奇异值增强缓解秩崩溃并提高数值稳定性。在10个欧洲葡萄牙语领域上对11种合并算法进行了基准测试。

Result: 提出的方法在欧洲葡萄牙语上优于全微调，同时在单一模型中保持了分布外泛化能力。评估了领域内准确性、分布偏移下的鲁棒性，以及英语和多语言性能。

Conclusion: 模型合并是多领域ASR的有效可扩展方案，BoostedTSV-M算法通过改进TSV-M解决了秩崩溃问题，在保持泛化能力的同时实现了优于全微调的性能。

Abstract: Model merging is a scalable alternative to multi-task training that combines the capabilities of multiple specialised models into a single model. This is particularly attractive for large speech foundation models, which are typically adapted through domain-specific fine-tuning, resulting in multiple customised checkpoints, for which repeating full fine-tuning when new data becomes available is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we study model merging for multi-domain ASR and benchmark 11 merging algorithms for 10 European Portuguese domains, evaluating in-domain accuracy, robustness under distribution shift, as well as English and multilingual performance. We further propose BoostedTSV-M, a new merging algorithm based on TSV-M that mitigates rank collapse via singular-value boosting and improves numerical stability. Overall, our approach outperforms full fine-tuning on European Portuguese while preserving out-of-distribution generalisation in a single model.

</details>


### [129] [DiSCTT: Consensus-Guided Self-Curriculum for Efficient Test-Time Adaptation in Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05357)
*Mohammad Mahdi Moradi,Sudhir Mudur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiSCTT：基于难度感知和共识引导的自课程框架，通过实例级认知不确定性估计动态分配测试时优化策略，提高大语言模型推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法对所有输入应用统一的优化目标，导致在异构推理问题上效率低下或适应不稳定。需要一种能够根据实例难度动态调整优化策略的方法。

Method: 提出DiSCTT框架：1) 从采样的推理轨迹中估计实例级认知不确定性；2) 高共识输入使用监督微调，以多数同意的解决方案作为伪标签；3) 低共识输入使用强化学习，采用共识正则化目标，在相关性约束下鼓励多样性。

Result: 在广泛的数学和通用推理基准测试中，DiSCTT始终优于强大的测试时适应基线，实现了更高的准确率、更低的方差，并显著减少了计算和训练时间。

Conclusion: 明确考虑实例难度和不确定性能够实现更稳定、高效和有效的推理模型测试时适应。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation offers a promising avenue for improving reasoning performance in large language models without additional supervision, but existing approaches often apply a uniform optimization objective across all inputs, leading to inefficient or unstable adaptation on heterogeneous reasoning problems. We propose DiSCTT, a difficulty-aware, consensus-guided self-curriculum framework that dynamically allocates test-time optimization strategies based on instance-level epistemic uncertainty estimated from agreement among sampled reasoning trajectories. Inputs with high consensus are consolidated via supervised fine-tuning using majority-agreed solutions as pseudo-labels, while low-consensus inputs are optimized via reinforcement learning with a consensus-regularized objective that encourages diversity under relevance constraints. Across a broad suite of mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks, DiSCTT consistently outperforms strong test-time adaptation baselines, achieving higher accuracy with reduced variance and substantially lower computation and wall-clock training times. These results demonstrate that explicitly accounting for instance difficulty and uncertainty enables more stable, efficient, and effective test-time adaptation for reasoning models.

</details>


### [130] [Progressive Residual Warmup for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05369)
*Tianhao Chen,Xin Xu,Lu Yin,Hao Chen,Yang Wang,Shizhe Diao,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Progressive Residual Warmup (ProRes)方法，通过渐进式残差预热实现"浅层先学"的Transformer预训练策略，提升训练稳定性、收敛速度和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构作为大语言模型的核心，其预训练稳定性和收敛速度至关重要。考虑到层间顺序堆叠的逻辑依赖关系，需要一种机制让浅层先稳定学习，深层再逐步参与。

Method: 提出ProRes方法：将每层的残差连接乘以一个从0到1逐渐增大的标量系数，深层需要更长的预热步数。这种渐进式预热实现了"浅层先学"的哲学，让深层等待浅层进入稳定状态后再参与学习。

Result: 在不同模型规模、归一化和初始化方案下的预训练实验表明，ProRes不仅能稳定预训练过程，还引入了独特的优化轨迹，实现了更快的收敛速度、更强的泛化能力和更好的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ProRes通过渐进式残差预热机制有效解决了Transformer预训练的稳定性问题，同时提升了收敛速度和最终模型质量，为大规模语言模型训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer architectures serve as the backbone for most modern Large Language Models, therefore their pretraining stability and convergence speed are of central concern. Motivated by the logical dependency of sequentially stacked layers, we propose Progressive Residual Warmup (ProRes) for language model pretraining. ProRes implements an "early layer learns first" philosophy by multiplying each layer's residual with a scalar that gradually warms up from 0 to 1, with deeper layers taking longer warmup steps. In this way, deeper layers wait for early layers to settle into a more stable regime before contributing to learning. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ProRes through pretraining experiments across various model scales, as well as normalization and initialization schemes. Comprehensive analysis shows that ProRes not only stabilizes pretraining but also introduces a unique optimization trajectory, leading to faster convergence, stronger generalization and better downstream performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/dandingsky/ProRes.

</details>


### [131] [An Exploration-Analysis-Disambiguation Reasoning Framework for Word Sense Disambiguation with Low-Parameter LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05400)
*Deshan Sumanathilaka,Nicholas Micallef,Julian Hough*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 低参数LLM通过推理驱动的微调策略在词义消歧任务上达到与GPT-4-Turbo相当的性能，显著降低计算和能耗需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词义消歧是NLP的关键挑战，特别是处理罕见或领域特定词义时。虽然大参数LLM（如GPT-4-Turbo）在WSD上表现优异，但其计算和能耗需求限制了可扩展性。本研究探索低参数LLM（<4B参数）是否能够通过推理驱动的微调策略达到可比性能。

Method: 使用增强的FEWS数据集（包含半自动、富含推理的标注），对8个小型开源LLM（如Gemma和Qwen）进行微调。采用思维链推理与邻词分析相结合的方法，强调推理驱动的词义识别。

Result: Gemma-3-4B和Qwen-3-4B模型在FEWS数据集上持续超越所有中等参数基线和最先进模型，并在未见词义上表现出鲁棒泛化能力。在"Fool Me If You Can"数据集上的评估证实了无需任务特定微调的强跨域适应性。基于思维链的推理与邻词分析相结合的方法在零样本设置下达到与GPT-4-Turbo相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的推理中心微调，低参数LLM能够提供准确的词义消歧，同时显著降低计算和能耗需求，为资源受限环境中的高效WSD提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) remains a key challenge in Natural Language Processing (NLP), especially when dealing with rare or domain-specific senses that are often misinterpreted. While modern high-parameter Large Language Models (LLMs) such as GPT-4-Turbo have shown state-of-the-art WSD performance, their computational and energy demands limit scalability. This study investigates whether low-parameter LLMs (<4B parameters) can achieve comparable results through fine-tuning strategies that emphasize reasoning-driven sense identification. Using the FEWS dataset augmented with semi-automated, rationale-rich annotations, we fine-tune eight small-scale open-source LLMs (e.g. Gemma and Qwen). Our results reveal that Chain-of-Thought (CoT)-based reasoning combined with neighbour-word analysis achieves performance comparable to GPT-4-Turbo in zero-shot settings. Importantly, Gemma-3-4B and Qwen-3-4B models consistently outperform all medium-parameter baselines and state-of-the-art models on FEWS, with robust generalization to unseen senses. Furthermore, evaluation on the unseen "Fool Me If You Can'' dataset confirms strong cross-domain adaptability without task-specific fine-tuning. This work demonstrates that with carefully crafted reasoning-centric fine-tuning, low-parameter LLMs can deliver accurate WSD while substantially reducing computational and energy demands.

</details>


### [132] [FlashAttention-4: Algorithm and Kernel Pipelining Co-Design for Asymmetric Hardware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05451)
*Ted Zadouri,Markus Hoehnerbach,Jay Shah,Timmy Liu,Vijay Thakkar,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashAttention-4针对Blackwell GPU架构优化注意力计算，通过异步MMA操作、软件模拟指数函数、利用张量内存等技术，相比cuDNN和Triton实现显著加速，并在CuTe-DSL中实现快速编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI行业快速转向Blackwell架构GPU（如B200和GB200），这些新硬件表现出非对称扩展特性：张量核心吞吐量翻倍，但其他功能单元（共享内存带宽、指数单元）扩展较慢或保持不变。FlashAttention-3主要针对Hopper架构优化，无法充分利用Blackwell的新特性，需要新的优化方法来应对这些变化的瓶颈。

Method: 1. 重新设计流水线，利用完全异步的MMA操作和更大的瓦片尺寸；2. 软件模拟指数函数和条件softmax重缩放，减少非矩阵乘法操作；3. 利用张量内存和2-CTA MMA模式减少共享内存流量和后向传播中的原子加法操作。此外，完全在嵌入Python的CuTe-DSL中实现，相比传统C++模板方法实现20-30倍的编译加速。

Result: 在B200 GPU上使用BF16精度，FlashAttention-4相比cuDNN 9.13实现最高1.3倍加速，相比Triton实现最高2.7倍加速，达到1613 TFLOPs/s（71%利用率）。

Conclusion: FlashAttention-4成功解决了Blackwell GPU架构中的新瓶颈，通过针对性的算法优化和高效的CuTe-DSL实现，显著提升了注意力计算的性能，同时大幅减少了编译时间，为大规模语言模型和长上下文应用提供了高效的注意力计算解决方案。

Abstract: Attention, as a core layer of the ubiquitous Transformer architecture, is the bottleneck for large language models and long-context applications. While FlashAttention-3 optimized attention for Hopper GPUs through asynchronous execution and warp specialization, it primarily targets the H100 architecture. The AI industry has rapidly transitioned to deploying Blackwell-based systems such as the B200 and GB200, which exhibit fundamentally different performance characteristics due to asymmetric hardware scaling: tensor core throughput doubles while other functional units (shared memory bandwidth, exponential units) scale more slowly or remain unchanged. We develop several techniques to address these shifting bottlenecks on Blackwell GPUs: (1) redesigned pipelines that exploit fully asynchronous MMA operations and larger tile sizes, (2) software-emulated exponential and conditional softmax rescaling that reduces non-matmul operations, and (3) leveraging tensor memory and the 2-CTA MMA mode to reduce shared memory traffic and atomic adds in the backward pass. We demonstrate that our method, FlashAttention-4, achieves up to 1.3$\times$ speedup over cuDNN 9.13 and 2.7$\times$ over Triton on B200 GPUs with BF16, reaching up to 1613 TFLOPs/s (71% utilization). Beyond algorithmic innovations, we implement FlashAttention-4 entirely in CuTe-DSL embedded in Python, achieving 20-30$\times$ faster compile times compared to traditional C++ template-based approaches while maintaining full expressivity.

</details>


### [133] [DEBISS: a Corpus of Individual, Semi-structured and Spoken Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05459)
*Klaywert Danillo Ferreira de Souza,David Eduardo Pereira,Cláudio E. C. Campelo,Larissa Lucena Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DEBISS语料库：一个包含口语和个体辩论的半结构化辩论语料库，具有多种NLP任务标注


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论在日常生活中有广泛应用，但由于辩论的多样性（应用场景、结构、格式），开发能够涵盖这些变化的语料库具有挑战性，现有辩论语料库稀缺

Method: 构建DEBISS语料库：收集口语和个体辩论，采用半结构化特征设计，包含多种NLP任务标注，如语音转文本、说话人分离、论点挖掘和辩论者质量评估

Result: 创建了一个具有广泛NLP任务标注的辩论语料库，填补了现有辩论语料库稀缺的空白

Conclusion: DEBISS语料库为辩论相关研究提供了有价值的资源，支持多种NLP任务，有助于推动辩论分析和理解的研究进展

Abstract: The process of debating is essential in our daily lives, whether in studying, work activities, simple everyday discussions, political debates on TV, or online discussions on social networks. The range of uses for debates is broad. Due to the diverse applications, structures, and formats of debates, developing corpora that account for these variations can be challenging, and the scarcity of debate corpora in the state of the art is notable. For this reason, the current research proposes the DEBISS corpus: a collection of spoken and individual debates with semi-structured features. With a broad range of NLP task annotations, such as speech-to-text, speaker diarization, argument mining, and debater quality assessment.

</details>


### [134] [NCTB-QA: A Large-Scale Bangla Educational Question Answering Dataset and Benchmarking Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05462)
*Abrar Eyasir,Tahsin Ahmed,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对低资源语言阅读理解系统中不可回答问题处理不可靠的问题，构建了NCTB-QA数据集——一个包含87,805个问答对的大型孟加拉语数据集，其中平衡分布了可回答(57.25%)和不可回答(42.75%)问题，并包含对抗性设计的干扰项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（特别是孟加拉语）的阅读理解系统在处理不可回答问题时表现不可靠，当正确答案不在上下文中时容易产生错误响应。现有孟加拉语数据集缺乏平衡的不可回答问题分布，限制了系统在真实教育场景中的鲁棒性评估。

Method: 从孟加拉国国家课程与教科书委员会出版的50本教科书中提取87,805个问答对构建NCTB-QA数据集，确保可回答(57.25%)和不可回答(42.75%)问题的平衡分布。数据集包含对抗性设计的实例，其中包含合理的干扰项。使用三种基于Transformer的模型(BERT、RoBERTa、ELECTRA)进行基准测试，并通过微调评估性能改进。

Result: 微调后模型性能显著提升：BERT的F1分数相对提升313%(从0.150到0.620)。所有模型的BERTScore语义答案质量也显著提高。研究结果表明，领域特定的微调对于低资源环境下的鲁棒性能至关重要。

Conclusion: NCTB-QA为孟加拉语教育问答提供了一个具有挑战性的基准，证明了平衡的不可回答问题分布和对抗性设计对于评估系统鲁棒性的重要性。该研究强调了领域特定微调在低资源语言处理中的关键作用。

Abstract: Reading comprehension systems for low-resource languages face significant challenges in handling unanswerable questions. These systems tend to produce unreliable responses when correct answers are absent from context. To solve this problem, we introduce NCTB-QA, a large-scale Bangla question answering dataset comprising 87,805 question-answer pairs extracted from 50 textbooks published by Bangladesh's National Curriculum and Textbook Board. Unlike existing Bangla datasets, NCTB-QA maintains a balanced distribution of answerable (57.25%) and unanswerable (42.75%) questions. NCTB-QA also includes adversarially designed instances containing plausible distractors. We benchmark three transformer-based models (BERT, RoBERTa, ELECTRA) and demonstrate substantial improvements through fine-tuning. BERT achieves 313% relative improvement in F1 score (0.150 to 0.620). Semantic answer quality measured by BERTScore also increases significantly across all models. Our results establish NCTB-QA as a challenging benchmark for Bangla educational question answering. This study demonstrates that domain-specific fine-tuning is critical for robust performance in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [135] [Leveraging LLM Parametric Knowledge for Fact Checking without Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05471)
*Artem Vazhentsev,Maria Marina,Daniil Moskovskiy,Sergey Pletenev,Mikhail Seleznyov,Mikhail Salnikov,Elena Tutubalina,Vasily Konovalov,Irina Nikishina,Alexander Panchenko,Viktor Moskvoretskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无需检索的事实核查任务，开发INTRA方法利用内部模型表示实现最先进性能，建立无需检索的事实核查作为有前景的研究方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检索的事实核查方法受限于检索错误和外部数据可用性，未能充分利用LLMs内在的事实核查能力，需要探索不依赖外部检索的事实核查方法

Method: 提出无需检索的事实核查任务，引入综合评估框架测试泛化能力，开发INTRA方法利用内部模型表示之间的交互作用进行事实核查

Result: 在9个数据集、18种方法和3个模型上的实验表明，基于logit的方法通常表现不佳，而利用内部表示的方法更好；INTRA方法实现了最先进的性能并具有强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: 无需检索的事实核查是一个有前景的研究方向，可以补充基于检索的框架，提高可扩展性，并能在训练中作为奖励信号或集成到生成过程中

Abstract: Trustworthiness is a core research challenge for agentic AI systems built on Large Language Models (LLMs). To enhance trust, natural language claims from diverse sources, including human-written text, web content, and model outputs, are commonly checked for factuality by retrieving external knowledge and using an LLM to verify the faithfulness of claims to the retrieved evidence. As a result, such methods are constrained by retrieval errors and external data availability, while leaving the models intrinsic fact-verification capabilities largely unused. We propose the task of fact-checking without retrieval, focusing on the verification of arbitrary natural language claims, independent of their source. To study this setting, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework focused on generalization, testing robustness to (i) long-tail knowledge, (ii) variation in claim sources, (iii) multilinguality, and (iv) long-form generation. Across 9 datasets, 18 methods and 3 models, our experiments indicate that logit-based approaches often underperform compared to those that leverage internal model representations. Building on this finding, we introduce INTRA, a method that exploits interactions between internal representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization. More broadly, our work establishes fact-checking without retrieval as a promising research direction that can complement retrieval-based frameworks, improve scalability, and enable the use of such systems as reward signals during training or as components integrated into the generation process.

</details>


### [136] [Reasoning Theater: Disentangling Model Beliefs from Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05488)
*Siddharth Boppana,Annabel Ma,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Eric Bigelow,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大型推理模型存在"表演性思维链"现象：模型在得出最终答案后仍继续生成推理过程，而早期激活探针能比思维链监控器更早解码答案，尤其是在简单任务中。研究通过激活探针、早期强制回答和思维链监控三种方法对比分析，发现探针引导的早期退出能显著减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索大型语言模型在思维链推理中的真实认知状态与表面生成行为之间的差异。研究者怀疑模型可能存在"表演性推理"现象，即模型在内部已确定答案后仍继续生成看似推理的文本，这引发了关于模型真实推理能力与学习到的推理模式之间的本质问题。

Method: 研究方法采用三种对比分析技术：1) 激活探针：从模型中间层激活中解码最终答案；2) 早期强制回答：在思维链中途强制模型输出答案；3) 思维链监控器：监控推理过程中的置信度变化。实验在两个大型模型（DeepSeek-R1 671B和GPT-OSS 120B）上进行，覆盖不同难度任务（简单回忆型MMLU问题和困难多跳推理GPQA-Diamond问题）。

Result: 研究结果显示：1) 对于简单MMLU问题，模型在思维链早期（远早于监控器能判断时）的激活中就可解码最终答案；2) 对于困难GPQA-Diamond问题，模型表现出真正的推理过程；3) 转折点（如回溯、"顿悟"时刻）主要出现在探针显示信念大幅变化时；4) 探针引导的早期退出能在MMLU上减少80%的token，在GPQA-Diamond上减少30%的token，同时保持相似准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明大型语言模型存在"表演性思维链"现象，特别是在简单任务中。激活探针能有效检测这种表演性推理，并实现自适应计算。转折点行为与真实的认知不确定性相关，而非单纯的"推理剧场"。该研究为理解模型内部推理过程提供了新视角，并为优化推理效率提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We provide evidence of performative chain-of-thought (CoT) in reasoning models, where a model becomes strongly confident in its final answer, but continues generating tokens without revealing its internal belief. Our analysis compares activation probing, early forced answering, and a CoT monitor across two large models (DeepSeek-R1 671B & GPT-OSS 120B) and find task difficulty-specific differences: The model's final answer is decodable from activations far earlier in CoT than a monitor is able to say, especially for easy recall-based MMLU questions. We contrast this with genuine reasoning in difficult multihop GPQA-Diamond questions. Despite this, inflection points (e.g., backtracking, 'aha' moments) occur almost exclusively in responses where probes show large belief shifts, suggesting these behaviors track genuine uncertainty rather than learned "reasoning theater." Finally, probe-guided early exit reduces tokens by up to 80% on MMLU and 30% on GPQA-Diamond with similar accuracy, positioning attention probing as an efficient tool for detecting performative reasoning and enabling adaptive computation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [SearchGym: A Modular Infrastructure for Cross-Platform Benchmarking and Hybrid Search Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04402)
*Jerome Tze-Hou Hsu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchGym是一个模块化基础设施，用于跨平台基准测试和混合搜索编排，通过解耦数据表示、嵌入策略和检索逻辑，实现可组合配置和完美可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG工具包在实验原型与生产就绪系统之间存在根本差距，需要一种能够支持跨平台基准测试和混合搜索编排的基础设施。

Method: 设计SearchGym模块化基础设施，将数据表示、嵌入策略和检索逻辑解耦为状态化抽象：Dataset、VectorSet和App，并引入组合配置代数，允许从分层配置合成整个系统。

Result: 在LitSearch专家标注基准测试中，SearchGym实现了70%的Top-100检索率，并分析了混合检索管道中的"Top-k认知"，发现语义排序和结构化过滤的最优顺序高度依赖于过滤强度。

Conclusion: SearchGym揭示了通用性与可优化性之间的设计张力，展示了工程优化作为揭示跨异构领域信息检索中因果机制工具的潜力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has created a proliferation of toolkits, yet a fundamental gap remains between experimental prototypes and robust, production-ready systems. We present SearchGym, a modular infrastructure designed for cross-platform benchmarking and hybrid search orchestration. Unlike existing model-centric frameworks, SearchGym decouples data representation, embedding strategies, and retrieval logic into stateful abstractions: Dataset, VectorSet, and App. This separation enables a Compositional Config Algebra, allowing designers to synthesize entire systems from hierarchical configurations while ensuring perfect reproducibility. Moreover, we analyze the "Top-$k$ Cognizance" in hybrid retrieval pipelines, demonstrating that the optimal sequence of semantic ranking and structured filtering is highly dependent on filter strength. Evaluated on the LitSearch expert-annotated benchmark, SearchGym achieves a 70% Top-100 retrieval rate. SearchGym reveals a design tension between generalizability and optimizability, presenting the potential where engineering optimization may serve as a tool for uncovering the causal mechanisms inherent in information retrieval across heterogeneous domains. An open-source implementation of SearchGym is available at: https://github.com/JeromeTH/search-gym

</details>


### [138] [Signal in the Noise: Decoding the Reality of Airline Service Quality with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04404)
*Ahmed Dawoud,Osama El-Shamy,Ahmed Habashy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 验证LLM框架从非结构化在线评论中提取航空服务问题细粒度洞察的有效性，发现埃及航空存在运营感知脱节现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务质量指标难以捕捉非结构化在线反馈中隐藏的乘客满意度驱动因素，需要更有效的分析工具来提取细粒度洞察

Method: 使用多阶段LLM管道分析16,000多条TripAdvisor评论（埃及航空和阿联酋航空，2016-2025年），将服务问题分类为36个具体类别

Result: 发现埃及航空存在"运营感知脱节"：尽管报告有运营改进，但2022年后乘客满意度骤降至2.0以下；识别出传统指标遗漏的具体驱动因素，如中断期间的沟通问题和员工行为；确定了关键旅游市场的情绪恶化

Conclusion: LLM框架作为强大的诊断工具，通过将非结构化乘客反馈转化为可操作的战略情报，超越了传统调查方法，对航空和旅游业具有重要价值

Abstract: Traditional service quality metrics often fail to capture the nuanced drivers of passenger satisfaction hidden within unstructured online feedback. This study validates a Large Language Model (LLM) framework designed to extract granular insights from such data. Analyzing over 16,000 TripAdvisor reviews for EgyptAir and Emirates (2016-2025), the study utilizes a multi-stage pipeline to categorize 36 specific service issues. The analysis uncovers a stark "operational perception disconnect" for EgyptAir: despite reported operational improvements, passenger satisfaction plummeted post-2022 (ratings < 2.0). Our approach identified specific drivers missed by conventional metrics-notably poor communication during disruptions and staff conduct-and pinpointed critical sentiment erosion in key tourism markets. These findings confirm the framework's efficacy as a powerful diagnostic tool, surpassing traditional surveys by transforming unstructured passenger voices into actionable strategic intelligence for the airline and tourism sectors.

</details>


### [139] [Still Fresh? Evaluating Temporal Drift in Retrieval Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04532)
*Nathan Kuissi,Suraj Subrahmanyan,Nandan Thakur,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究信息检索基准在技术语料库随时间变化时的稳定性，发现FreshStack基准在语料库漂移后仍保持较好的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IR基准遵循Cranfield范式，依赖静态预定义语料库，但技术语料库的时间变化（如API废弃、代码重组）可能导致基准过时，需要研究语料库漂移对技术检索基准的影响

Method: 使用FreshStack技术检索基准，分析2024年10月和2025年10月两个独立的语料库快照，研究LangChain相关查询的文档迁移情况，并比较检索模型在两个快照上的准确性

Result: 2024年的查询中除一个外，其余在2025年语料库中仍完全支持，相关文档从LangChain迁移到LlamaIndex等竞争对手仓库；检索模型在两个快照上的排名仅有微小变化，Recall@50的Kendall τ相关性高达0.978

Conclusion: 使用随时间演化的语料库重新评估的检索基准仍能保持可靠的检索评估能力，技术检索基准对语料库漂移具有较好的鲁棒性

Abstract: Information retrieval (IR) benchmarks typically follow the Cranfield paradigm, relying on static and predefined corpora. However, temporal changes in technical corpora, such as API deprecations and code reorganizations, can render existing benchmarks stale. In our work, we investigate how temporal corpus drift affects FreshStack, a retrieval benchmark focused on technical domains. We examine two independent corpus snapshots of FreshStack from October 2024 and October 2025 to answer questions about LangChain. Our analysis shows that all but one query posed in 2024 remain fully supported by the 2025 corpus, as relevant documents "migrate" from LangChain to competitor repositories, such as LlamaIndex. Next, we compare the accuracy of retrieval models on both snapshots and observe only minor shifts in model rankings, with overall strong correlation of up to 0.978 Kendall $τ$ at Recall@50. These results suggest that retrieval benchmarks re-judged with evolving temporal corpora can remain reliable for retrieval evaluation. We publicly release all our artifacts at https://github.com/fresh-stack/driftbench.

</details>


### [140] [Scaling Laws for Reranking in Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04816)
*Rahul Seetharaman,Aman Bansal,Hamed Zamani,Kaustubh Dhole*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究重排序器的缩放规律，发现性能遵循可预测的幂律，可通过小规模实验准确预测大规模模型的NDCG等指标性能，为工业级检索系统提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放规律研究主要关注自然语言生成和密集检索等任务，但缺乏对多阶段检索系统中重排序阶段的系统性研究。重排序作为检索系统的最终且最具影响力的步骤，其缩放行为尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过分析三种主流重排序范式（点对、成对、列表）在不同模型大小和数据预算下的性能，使用交叉编码器重排序器进行详细案例研究，验证性能是否遵循可预测的幂律规律。

Result: 实验表明重排序性能确实遵循可预测的幂律规律，NDCG、MAP等下游指标表现出可靠的缩放行为，可通过小规模模型（如400M参数）准确预测大规模模型（如1B参数）的性能，而对比熵和MRR等指标在某些情况下不遵循可预测的缩放模式。

Conclusion: 本研究首次建立了重排序的缩放原则，为构建工业级检索系统提供了可操作的见解，能够显著节省计算资源，通过小规模实验准确预测大规模模型的性能。

Abstract: Scaling laws have been observed across a wide range of tasks, such as natural language generation and dense retrieval, where performance follows predictable patterns as model size, data, and compute grow. However, these scaling laws are insufficient for understanding the scaling behavior of multi-stage retrieval systems, which typically include a reranking stage. In large-scale multi-stage retrieval systems, reranking is the final and most influential step before presenting a ranked list of items to the end user. In this work, we present the first systematic study of scaling laws for rerankers by analyzing performance across model sizes and data budgets for three popular paradigms: pointwise, pairwise, and listwise reranking. Using a detailed case study with cross-encoder rerankers, we demonstrate that performance follows a predictable power law. This regularity allows us to accurately forecast the performance of larger models for some metrics more than others using smaller-scale experiments, offering a robust methodology for saving significant computational resources. For example, we accurately estimate the NDCG of a 1B-parameter model by training and evaluating only smaller models (up to 400M parameters), in both in-domain as well as out-of-domain settings. Our experiments encompass span several loss functions, models and metrics and demonstrate that downstream metrics like NDCG, MAP (Mean Avg Precision) show reliable scaling behavior and can be forecasted accurately at scale, while highlighting the limitations of metrics like Contrastive Entropy and MRR (Mean Reciprocal Rank) which do not follow predictable scaling behavior in all instances. Our results establish scaling principles for reranking and provide actionable insights for building industrial-grade retrieval systems.

</details>


### [141] [Detecting RAG Advertisements Across Advertising Styles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04925)
*Sebastian Heineking,Wilhelm Pertsch,Ines Zelch,Janek Bevendorff,Benno Stein,Matthias Hagen,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM生成的"原生广告"检测问题，开发了广告风格分类法，评估了不同检测方法在广告风格变化下的鲁棒性，发现基于实体识别的模型效果最好但需要轻量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RAG系统中能够生成将有机回答与上下文相关广告混合的"生成原生广告"，现有数据集无法反映营销文献中讨论的广告风格多样性，需要研究自动检测方法及其在广告风格变化下的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 开发了基于明确性和诉求类型的LLM广告风格分类法；2) 模拟广告商通过改变广告风格来规避检测的场景；3) 评估多种广告检测方法在风格变化下的鲁棒性，包括基于实体识别的模型、随机森林和SVM等轻量级模型。

Result: 基于实体识别的模型在检测含广告的响应方面非常有效，且对广告风格变化具有较好的鲁棒性。轻量级模型（随机森林、SVM）在风格变化下表现脆弱，而广告拦截需要在低资源终端设备上运行，因此需要进一步研究高效实用的方法。

Conclusion: 虽然基于实体识别的模型能有效检测生成广告且对风格变化鲁棒，但由于需要在低资源设备上运行，轻量级模型的脆弱性凸显了需要进一步研究高效实用的生成广告拦截方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable a new form of advertising for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in which organic responses are blended with contextually relevant ads. The prospect of such "generated native ads" has sparked interest in whether they can be detected automatically. Existing datasets, however, do not reflect the diversity of advertising styles discussed in the marketing literature. In this paper, we (1) develop a taxonomy of advertising styles for LLMs, combining the style dimensions of explicitness and type of appeal, (2) simulate that advertisers may attempt to evade detection by changing their advertising style, and (3) evaluate a variety of ad-detection approaches with respect to their robustness under these changes. Expanding previous work on ad detection, we train models that use entity recognition to exactly locate an ad in an LLM response and find them to be both very effective at detecting responses with ads and largely robust to changes in the advertising style. Since ad blocking will be performed on low-resource end-user devices, we include lightweight models like random forests and SVMs in our evaluation. These models, however, are brittle under such changes, highlighting the need for further efficiency-oriented research for a practical approach to blocking of generated ads.

</details>


### [142] [Debiasing Sequential Recommendation with Time-aware Inverse Propensity Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04986)
*Sirui Huang,Jing Long,Qian Li,Guandong Xu,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了时间感知逆倾向评分（TIPS）方法，用于解决序列推荐中的选择偏差和曝光偏差问题，通过考虑序列依赖性和时间动态性来更准确地估计用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐方法主要依赖显式交互（如点击或购买），而忽略了物品曝光信息。这导致选择偏差（曝光但未点击的物品被误判为不感兴趣）和曝光偏差（未曝光的物品被视为无关）。传统逆倾向评分（IPS）方法是静态的，无法捕捉用户行为的序列依赖性和时间动态性。

Method: 提出了时间感知逆倾向评分（TIPS）方法，与传统静态IPS不同，TIPS能够有效考虑序列依赖性和时间动态性，从而更准确地捕捉用户偏好。该方法可作为插件应用于各种序列推荐器。

Result: 大量实验表明，TIPS作为插件能够持续提升各种序列推荐器的推荐性能。

Conclusion: TIPS通过考虑序列依赖性和时间动态性，有效解决了序列推荐中的选择偏差和曝光偏差问题，显著提升了推荐性能，可作为通用插件应用于现有序列推荐系统。

Abstract: Sequential Recommendation (SR) predicts users next interactions by modeling the temporal order of their historical behaviors. Existing approaches, including traditional sequential models and generative recommenders, achieve strong performance but primarily rely on explicit interactions such as clicks or purchases while overlooking item exposures. This ignorance introduces selection bias, where exposed but unclicked items are misinterpreted as disinterest, and exposure bias, where unexposed items are treated as irrelevant. Effectively addressing these biases requires distinguishing between items that were "not exposed" and those that were "not of interest", which cannot be reliably inferred from correlations in historical data. Counterfactual reasoning provides a natural solution by estimating user preferences under hypothetical exposure, and Inverse Propensity Scoring (IPS) is a common tool for such estimation. However, conventional IPS methods are static and fail to capture the sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics of user behavior. To overcome these limitations, we propose Time aware Inverse Propensity Scoring (TIPS). Unlike traditional static IPS, TIPS effectively accounts for sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics, thereby capturing user preferences more accurately. Extensive experiments show that TIPS consistently enhances recommendation performance as a plug-in for various sequential recommenders. Our code will be publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [143] [Core-based Hierarchies for Efficient GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05207)
*Jakir Hossain,Ahmet Erdem Sarıyüce*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类，解决稀疏知识图谱中社区检测的非可重复性问题，构建确定性、密度感知的层次结构，提升GraphRAG在全局意义构建任务中的效果和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量的RAG方法在需要跨多文档推理的全局意义构建任务上表现不佳。GraphRAG通过知识图谱和层次社区结构解决此问题，但当前基于Leiden聚类的方法在稀疏知识图谱上存在非可重复性问题，因为模块度优化会产生指数级数量的近最优划分。

Method: 提出用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类，在稀疏知识图谱上生成确定性、密度感知的层次结构。引入轻量级启发式方法，利用k-core层次构建大小受限、保持连通性的社区用于检索和摘要，并提出基于token预算的采样策略降低LLM成本。

Result: 在真实数据集（财务收益电话会议记录、新闻文章、播客）上评估，使用三种LLM生成答案，五位独立LLM法官进行头对头评估。结果表明，该方法在多个数据集和模型上一致提高了答案的全面性和多样性，同时减少了token使用量。

Conclusion: 基于k-core的GraphRAG是全局意义构建任务中有效且高效的框架，解决了现有方法在稀疏知识图谱上的可重复性问题，同时提升了检索和摘要的质量与效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge. However, existing vector-based methods often fail on global sensemaking tasks that require reasoning across many documents. GraphRAG addresses this by organizing documents into a knowledge graph with hierarchical communities that can be recursively summarized. Current GraphRAG approaches rely on Leiden clustering for community detection, but we prove that on sparse knowledge graphs, where average degree is constant and most nodes have low degree, modularity optimization admits exponentially many near-optimal partitions, making Leiden-based communities inherently non-reproducible. To address this, we propose replacing Leiden with k-core decomposition, which yields a deterministic, density-aware hierarchy in linear time. We introduce a set of lightweight heuristics that leverage the k-core hierarchy to construct size-bounded, connectivity-preserving communities for retrieval and summarization, along with a token-budget-aware sampling strategy that reduces LLM costs. We evaluate our methods on real-world datasets including financial earnings transcripts, news articles, and podcasts, using three LLMs for answer generation and five independent LLM judges for head-to-head evaluation. Across datasets and models, our approach consistently improves answer comprehensiveness and diversity while reducing token usage, demonstrating that k-core-based GraphRAG is an effective and efficient framework for global sensemaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [SarcasmMiner: A Dual-Track Post-Training Framework for Robust Audio-Visual Sarcasm Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05275)
*Zhu Li,Yongjian Chen,Huiyuan Lai,Xiyuan Gao,Shekhar Nayak,Matt Coler*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: SarcasmMiner：基于强化学习的后训练框架，通过结构化推理和双重轨迹蒸馏策略提升多模态讽刺检测性能，在MUStARD++数据集上F1从59.83%（零样本）提升至70.22%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测需要跨文本、声学和视觉线索解决语用不一致性，但现有基础模型在多模态推理中存在幻觉问题，需要提升推理鲁棒性

Method: 将讽刺检测重构为结构化推理任务，采用双重轨迹蒸馏策略：高质量教师轨迹初始化学生模型，完整轨迹集训练生成式奖励模型评估推理质量，使用解耦的准确性和推理质量奖励通过组相对策略优化优化学生模型

Result: 在MUStARD++数据集上，SarcasmMiner将F1分数从零样本的59.83%、监督微调的68.23%提升至70.22%，表明推理感知的奖励建模能同时提升性能和跨模态基础

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习后训练框架SarcasmMiner通过结构化推理和双重轨迹蒸馏有效减少多模态推理中的幻觉，推理感知的奖励建模策略显著提升了多模态讽刺检测的性能和跨模态基础能力

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires resolving pragmatic incongruity across textual, acoustic, and visual cues through cross-modal reasoning. To enable robust sarcasm reasoning with foundation models, we propose SarcasmMiner, a reinforcement learning based post-training framework that resists hallucination in multimodal reasoning. We reformulate sarcasm detection as structured reasoning and adopt a dual-track distillation strategy: high-quality teacher trajectories initialize the student model, while the full set of trajectories trains a generative reward model (GenRM) to evaluate reasoning quality. The student is optimized with group relative policy optimization (GRPO) using decoupled rewards for accuracy and reasoning quality. On MUStARD++, SarcasmMiner increases F1 from 59.83% (zero-shot), 68.23% (supervised finetuning) to 70.22%. These findings suggest that reasoning-aware reward modeling enhances both performance and multimodal grounding.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy for Causal Direction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04473)
*Harikrishnan N B,Shubham Bhilare,Aditi Kathpalia,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于字典模式熵（DPE）的新框架，利用算法信息论和香农信息论，通过最小化模式级不确定性来推断符号序列的因果方向，在合成数据中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号序列的因果方向发现具有挑战性，因为缺乏函数模型和噪声假设。现有方法难以处理符号序列的因果推断问题，需要一种能够识别驱动因果关系的特定子模式的新方法。

Method: 提出字典模式熵（DPE）框架，整合算法信息论和香农信息论。将因果关系解释为候选原因中紧凑、基于规则的模式的涌现，这些模式系统地约束了效应。构建方向特定的字典，使用基于熵的度量量化其影响，建立确定性模式结构和随机变异性之间的原则性联系。

Result: 在多种合成系统中（延迟比特翻转扰动、AR(1)耦合、1D斜帐篷映射、稀疏过程）表现可靠，优于或匹配基于AIT的竞争方法（ETC_E、ETC_P、LZ_P）。在生物和生态数据集中表现有竞争力，而替代方法在特定基因组设置中显示出优势。

Conclusion: 最小化模式级不确定性产生了一个稳健、可解释且广泛适用的因果发现框架，能够同时推断因果方向和驱动效应变化的特定子模式。

Abstract: Discovering causal direction from temporal observational data is particularly challenging for symbolic sequences, where functional models and noise assumptions are often unavailable. We propose a novel \emph{Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy ($DPE$)} framework that infers both the direction of causation and the specific subpatterns driving changes in the effect variable. The framework integrates \emph{Algorithmic Information Theory} (AIT) and \emph{Shannon Information Theory}. Causation is interpreted as the emergence of compact, rule based patterns in the candidate cause that systematically constrain the effect. $DPE$ constructs direction-specific dictionaries and quantifies their influence using entropy-based measures, enabling a principled link between deterministic pattern structure and stochastic variability. Causal direction is inferred via a minimum-uncertainty criterion, selecting the direction exhibiting stronger and more consistent pattern-driven organization. As summarized in Table 7, $DPE$ consistently achieves reliable performance across diverse synthetic systems, including delayed bit-flip perturbations, AR(1) coupling, 1D skew-tent maps, and sparse processes, outperforming or matching competing AIT-based methods ($ETC_E$, $ETC_P$, $LZ_P$). In biological and ecological datasets, performance is competitive, while alternative methods show advantages in specific genomic settings. Overall, the results demonstrate that minimizing pattern level uncertainty yields a robust, interpretable, and broadly applicable framework for causal discovery.

</details>


### [146] [Bayesian Modeling of Collatz Stopping Times: A Probabilistic Machine Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04479)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi,Matteo Bordoni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文从概率机器学习视角研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)在n≤10^7范围内的统计特性，开发了贝叶斯负二项回归和基于奇块分解的生成模型两种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)的统计分布特性，该函数表现出明显的偏斜、过度分散和算术异质性，需要从概率机器学习角度进行建模分析。

Method: 提出两种互补模型：1) 贝叶斯分层负二项回归(NB2-GLM)，使用log n和n mod 8作为协变量预测τ(n)，通过后验和后验预测分布量化不确定性；2) 基于奇块分解的机制生成近似：对奇数m，将3m+1分解为2^{K(m)}m'，随机化块长度并通过Dirichlet-multinomial更新校准。

Result: 在留出数据上，NB2-GLM模型比奇块生成器获得显著更高的预测似然；将块长度分布条件化到m mod 8上显著改善了生成器的分布拟合，表明低阶模结构是τ(n)异质性的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: Collatz总停止时间τ(n)具有复杂的统计特性，可以通过概率机器学习方法有效建模；低阶模结构（特别是模8）在解释τ(n)的异质性中起关键作用，贝叶斯回归模型在预测性能上优于基于机制的生成模型。

Abstract: We study the Collatz total stopping time $τ(n)$ over $n\le 10^7$ from a probabilistic machine learning viewpoint. Empirically, $τ(n)$ is a skewed and heavily overdispersed count with pronounced arithmetic heterogeneity. We develop two complementary models. First, a Bayesian hierarchical Negative Binomial regression (NB2-GLM) predicts $τ(n)$ from simple covariates ($\log n$ and residue class $n \bmod 8$), quantifying uncertainty via posterior and posterior predictive distributions. Second, we propose a mechanistic generative approximation based on the odd-block decomposition: for odd $m$, write $3m+1=2^{K(m)}m'$ with $m'$ odd and $K(m)=v_2(3m+1)\ge 1$; randomizing these block lengths yields a stochastic approximation calibrated via a Dirichlet-multinomial update. On held-out data, the NB2-GLM achieves substantially higher predictive likelihood than the odd-block generators. Conditioning the block-length distribution on $m\bmod 8$ markedly improves the generator's distributional fit, indicating that low-order modular structure is a key driver of heterogeneity in $τ(n)$.

</details>


### [147] [The Volterra signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04525)
*Paul P. Hager,Fabian N. Harang,Luca Pelizzari,Samy Tindel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Volterra签名作为历史依赖系统的显式特征表示，通过张量代数开发带时间核的输入路径，获得学习理论保证，在动态学习任务中优于经典路径签名基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理非马尔可夫时间序列的方法（如RNN、神经控制微分方程、Transformer）依赖隐式记忆机制，难以解释且长时程训练困难，需要更原则性的显式特征表示

Method: 提出Volterra签名VSig(x;K)，通过时间核K对输入路径x加权并展开到张量代数，利用Volterra-Chen恒等式建立理论保证，通过核技巧实现高效计算

Result: 证明了注入性（增强下的可识别性）和无限维路径空间上的通用逼近定理；对指数型核，Volterra签名可解张量代数中的线性状态空间ODE；在实际和合成数据上优于经典路径签名基线

Conclusion: Volterra签名作为历史依赖系统的显式特征表示，具有理论保证、计算可处理性和时间重参数化不变性，是数据科学中鲁棒的特征映射方法

Abstract: Modern approaches for learning from non-Markovian time series, such as recurrent neural networks, neural controlled differential equations or transformers, typically rely on implicit memory mechanisms that can be difficult to interpret or to train over long horizons. We propose the Volterra signature $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ as a principled, explicit feature representation for history-dependent systems. By developing the input path $x$ weighted by a temporal kernel $K$ into the tensor algebra, we leverage the associated Volterra--Chen identity to derive rigorous learning-theoretic guarantees. Specifically, we prove an injectivity statement (identifiability under augmentation) that leads to a universal approximation theorem on the infinite dimensional path space, which in certain cases is achieved by linear functionals of $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$. Moreover, we demonstrate applicability of the kernel trick by showing that the inner product associated with Volterra signatures admits a closed characterization via a two-parameter integral equation, enabling numerical methods from PDEs for computation. For a large class of exponential-type kernels, $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ solves a linear state-space ODE in the tensor algebra. Combined with inherent invariance to time reparameterization, these results position the Volterra signature as a robust, computationally tractable feature map for data science. We demonstrate its efficacy in dynamic learning tasks on real and synthetic data, where it consistently improves classical path signature baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Optimal Prediction-Augmented Algorithms for Testing Independence of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04635)
*Maryam Aliakbarpour,Alireza Azizi,Ria Stevens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增强分布测试框架的独立性检验方法，利用辅助预测信息在保持最坏情况有效性的同时显著提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非参数有限样本独立性检验存在样本复杂度高的问题，需要随支持集大小多项式增长。为了突破这些最坏情况限制，研究者希望利用辅助预测信息来提高检验效率。

Method: 采用增强分布测试框架，设计包含辅助预测信息的独立性检验器。该方法保持鲁棒性，无论预测质量如何都维持最坏情况有效性，同时在预测准确时显著提高样本效率。具体包括：1）针对离散分布的二元独立性检验器，能根据预测误差自适应降低样本复杂度；2）扩展到高维多变量设置，用于检验d个随机变量的独立性；3）匹配的最小最大下界证明。

Result: 提出的检验器在预测准确时能显著提高样本效率，同时保持最坏情况有效性。通过匹配的最小最大下界证明，这些检验器达到了最优的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 增强分布测试框架为独立性检验提供了新思路，通过利用辅助预测信息在保持鲁棒性的同时突破了传统最坏情况样本复杂度的限制，实现了更高效的检验方法。

Abstract: Independence testing is a fundamental problem in statistical inference: given samples from a joint distribution $p$ over multiple random variables, the goal is to determine whether $p$ is a product distribution or is $ε$-far from all product distributions in total variation distance. In the non-parametric finite-sample regime, this task is notoriously expensive, as the minimax sample complexity scales polynomially with the support size. In this work, we move beyond these worst-case limitations by leveraging the framework of \textit{augmented distribution testing}. We design independence testers that incorporate auxiliary, but potentially untrustworthy, predictive information. Our framework ensures that the tester remains robust, maintaining worst-case validity regardless of the prediction's quality, while significantly improving sample efficiency when the prediction is accurate. Our main contributions include: (i) a bivariate independence tester for discrete distributions that adaptively reduces sample complexity based on the prediction error; (ii) a generalization to the high-dimensional multivariate setting for testing the independence of $d$ random variables; and (iii) matching minimax lower bounds demonstrating that our testers achieve optimal sample complexity.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Optimal Individualized Decision Rules with Conditional Demographic Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05226)
*Wenhai Cui,Wen Su,Donglin Zeng,Xingqiu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种在个体化决策规则中融入人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束的新框架，通过扰动无约束最优规则实现高效计算，并在理论和实证上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化决策规则在个性化营销、医疗和公共政策等社会应用中日益普及，但基于有偏数据训练的算法可能对性别、种族或语言等敏感属性定义的少数亚群体造成不成比例的伤害，存在歧视性影响的伦理问题。

Method: 提出将人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束纳入最优个体化决策规则估计的新框架，通过证明在DP和CDP约束下的理论最优IDR可以通过对无约束最优IDR施加扰动获得，从而实现计算高效的解决方案。

Result: 理论上推导了策略价值和公平约束项的收敛率；通过综合模拟研究和俄勒冈健康保险实验的实证应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为在个体化决策规则中实现公平性提供了有效的理论和方法支持，能够缓解基于有偏数据训练的算法对少数群体的歧视性影响。

Abstract: Individualized decision rules (IDRs) have become increasingly prevalent in societal applications such as personalized marketing, healthcare, and public policy design. However, a critical ethical concern arises from the potential discriminatory effects of IDRs trained on biased data. These algorithms may disproportionately harm individuals from minority subgroups defined by sensitive attributes like gender, race, or language. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that incorporates demographic parity (DP) and conditional demographic parity (CDP) constraints into the estimation of optimal IDRs. We show that the theoretically optimal IDRs under DP and CDP constraints can be obtained by applying perturbations to the unconstrained optimal IDRs, enabling a computationally efficient solution. Theoretically, we derive convergence rates for both policy value and the fairness constraint term. The effectiveness of our methods is illustrated through comprehensive simulation studies and an empirical application to the Oregon Health Insurance Experiment.

</details>


### [150] [Bayes with No Shame: Admissibility Geometries of Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05335)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了四种不同的可容许性几何框架，证明了它们之间的分离定理，表明这些可容许性标准是成对不嵌套的，可容许性本质上是准则相对的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究序列和无分布推断中不同可容许性几何框架之间的关系，揭示它们之间的本质差异，证明不存在统一的"最优"概念，可容许性依赖于具体的准则。

Method: 通过几何分析和分离定理证明四种可容许性框架（Blackwell风险支配、任意时间有效的可容许性、边际覆盖有效性、Cesàro可接近性）是成对不嵌套的，每种框架都有不同的最优性证书。

Result: 证明了四种可容许性标准是几何不相容的，它们在不同的空间、偏序和性能度量上操作，鞅相干性对于Blackwell可容许性是必要的，对于任意时间有效的可容许性是必要且充分的，但对于覆盖有效性或CAA可容许性既不是必要的也不是充分的。

Conclusion: 可容许性是准则相对的，不存在统一的"最优"概念，不同的几何框架提供了不同的最优性证书，研究人员需要根据具体问题选择合适的可容许性准则。

Abstract: Four distinct admissibility geometries govern sequential and distribution-free inference: Blackwell risk dominance over convex risk sets, anytime-valid admissibility within the nonnegative supermartingale cone, marginal coverage validity over exchangeable prediction sets, and Cesàro approachability (CAA) admissibility, which reaches the risk-set boundary via approachability-style arguments rather than explicit priors. We prove a criterion separation theorem: the four classes of admissible procedures are pairwise non-nested. Each geometry carries a different certificate of optimality: a supporting-hyperplane prior (Blackwell), a nonnegative supermartingale (anytime-valid), an exchangeability rank (coverage), or a Cesàro steering argument (CAA). Martingale coherence is necessary for Blackwell admissibility and necessary and sufficient for anytime-valid admissibility within e-processes, but is not sufficient for Blackwell admissibility and is not necessary for coverage validity or CAA-admissibility. All four criteria share a common optimization template (minimize Bayesian risk subject to a feasibility constraint), but the constraint sets operate over different spaces, partial orders, and performance metrics, making them geometrically incompatible. Admissibility is irreducibly criterion-relative.

</details>


### [151] [Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05288)
*Luwei Wang,Nazir Lone,Sohan Seth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC）方法，以处理效应为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖无监督聚类，但监督聚类方法能针对特定关注结果识别可操作亚组，在医疗和政策评估中寻找具有相似特征的患者亚组对个性化决策至关重要

Method: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC），以处理效应为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组

Result: 在模拟数据集和国际卒中试验（IST）真实世界数据集上评估BSCC，验证了该框架的实际应用价值

Conclusion: BSCC能够有效识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组，为个性化医疗和政策评估中的决策提供支持

Abstract: Finding patient subgroups with similar characteristics is crucial for personalized decision-making in various disciplines such as healthcare and policy evaluation. While most existing approaches rely on unsupervised clustering methods, there is a growing trend toward using supervised clustering methods that identify operationalizable subgroups in the context of a specific outcome of interest. We propose Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering (BSCC), with treatment effect as outcome to guide the clustering process. BSCC identifies homogenous subgroups of individuals who are similar in their covariate profiles as well as their treatment effects. We evaluate BSCC on simulated datasets as well as real-world dataset from the third International Stroke Trial to assess the practical usefulness of the framework.

</details>


### [152] [On the Statistical Optimality of Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05340)
*Zineng Xu,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为经验风险最小化（ERM）决策树建立了全面的统计理论，包括高维回归和分类中的尖锐oracle不等式、最小化最优速率，并引入了适用于重尾噪声的鲁棒保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全局最优ERM决策树在计算上已可行且经验上成功，但其统计性能的严格理论保证仍然有限。需要为ERM树建立全面的统计理论，特别是在高维回归和分类的随机设计设置下。
