For honesty elicitation, sampling without a chat template, few-shot prompting, and fine-tuning on generic honesty data most reliably increase truthful responses. For lie detection, prompting the censored model to classify its own responses performs near an uncensored-model upper bound, and linear probes trained on unrelated data offer a cheaper alternative. The strongest honesty elicitation techniques also transfer to frontier open-weights models including DeepSeek R1. Notably, no technique fully eliminates false responses. We release all prompts, code, and transcripts.

</details>


### [269] [POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500)
*Zeju Qiu,Lixin Liu,Adrian Weller,Han Shi,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POET-X：一种可扩展且内存高效的谱保持训练框架，通过正交等价变换优化LLM权重矩阵，显著降低计算开销和内存消耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决POET框架虽然提供强大训练稳定性，但原始实现因密集矩阵乘法导致高内存消耗和计算开销的问题

Method: 提出POET-X作为POET的可扩展变体，通过正交等价变换显著降低计算成本，保持谱保持特性

Result: POET-X在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上成功预训练十亿参数级LLM，而标准AdamW在相同设置下内存不足；保持POET的泛化和稳定性优势，同时在吞吐量和内存效率上有显著提升

Conclusion: POET-X是一种高效且稳定的LLM训练框架，解决了大规模模型训练中的内存和计算瓶颈问题

Abstract: Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [How Professional Visual Artists are Negotiating Generative AI in the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04537)
*Harry H. Jiang,Jordan Taylor,William Agnew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对378名专业视觉艺术家的调查显示，大多数艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，通过拒绝策略抵制其在工作场所的应用，并报告了AI带来的负面职业影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在流行媒体和HCI研究中受到艺术家的强烈批评，但对其如何影响专业艺术家的工作场所和职业发展仍缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过调查专业视觉艺术家来了解生成式AI对其职业生涯和工作环境的实际影响。

Method: 对378名经过验证的专业视觉艺术家进行问卷调查，研究生成式AI（包括文本和视觉生成）如何影响他们的职业生涯和工作场所。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 大多数视觉艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，并通过各种拒绝策略来抵制其在工作场所的应用；(2) 艺术家环境中存在多种因素影响他们对生成式AI的使用，包括来自客户、老板和同行的压力；(3) 视觉艺术家普遍报告生成式AI对其工作场所产生负面影响，导致压力增加和工作机会减少。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者鼓励HCI研究人员更深入地考虑艺术家在工作场所不希望使用生成式AI的意愿，并建议未来研究应更多关注艺术家对AI技术的抵制和拒绝策略。

Abstract: Generative AI has been heavily critiqued by artists in both popular media and HCI scholarship. However, more work is needed to understand the impacts of generative AI on professional artists' workplaces and careers. In this paper, we conduct a survey of \textit{378 verified professional visual artists} about how generative AI has impacted their careers and workplaces. We find (1) most visual artists are strongly opposed to using generative AI (text or visual) and negotiate their inclusion in the workplace through a variety of \textit{refusal} strategies (2) there exist a range of factors in artists environments shaping their use of generative AI, including pressure from clients, bosses, and peers and (3) visual artists report overwhelmingly negative impacts of generative AI on their workplaces, leading to added stress and reduced job opportunities. In light of these findings, we encourage HCI researchers to contend more deeply with artists' desires not to use generative AI in the workplace.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond the Interface: Redefining UX for Society-in-the-Loop AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04552)
*Nahal Mafi,Sahar Maleki,Babak Rahimi Ardabili,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出AI人机协同系统中用户体验需超越前端可用性，涵盖后端性能、组织流程和决策结构，并基于实证研究建立包含准确性、操作延迟、适应时间和信任度的四维评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户体验框架针对确定性系统设计，无法捕捉AI人机协同系统中概率性输出和人机交互带来的社会技术动态变化，需要新的评估框架来理解这些复杂系统。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对269个利益相关者见解进行归纳性社会建构分析；2）部署操作性的HITL视频异常检测系统进行实证研究。

Result: 研究发现利益相关者通过风险、治理和组织能力等多维度体验AI；实验显示检测行为和警报路由直接影响人类监督和工作负荷；基于此建立了包含准确性（FPR/FNR）、操作延迟（响应时间）、适应时间（部署负担）和信任度（验证自动化量表）的四维社会技术指标框架。

Conclusion: 用户体验应重新定义为跨越基础设施和治理的多层结构，为评估嵌入复杂现实生态系统中的AI系统提供严谨基础，将UX从单纯的前端可用性扩展到整个社会技术系统。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly operate in decision-critical environments where probabilistic outputs and Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) interactions reshape user engagement. Traditional user experience (UX) frameworks, designed for deterministic systems, fail to capture these evolving sociotechnical dynamics. This paper argues that in AI-enabled HITL systems, UX must transcend frontend usability to encompass backend performance, organizational workflows, and decision making structures.
  We employ a mixed-methods approach, combining an inductive social construction analysis of 269 stakeholder insights with the deployment of an operational HITL video anomaly detection system. Our findings reveal that stakeholders experience AI through multifaceted themes: risk, governance, and organizational capacity. Experimental results further demonstrate how detection behavior and alert routing directly calibrate human oversight and workload. Grounded in these results, we formalize a new evaluative framework centered on four sociotechnical metrics: Accuracy (FPR/FNR), Operational Latency (response time), Adaptation Time (deployment burden), and Trust (validated automation scales). This framework redefines UX as a multi-layered construct spanning infrastructure and governance, providing a rigorous foundation for evaluating AI systems embedded within complex real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: a Spectrum of Interface Metaphors for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04613)
*Jianna So,Connie Cheng,Sonia Krishna Murthy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出将拟人化作为设计变量，通过创建从反拟人化到超拟人化的隐喻谱系，揭示LLM作为社会技术系统的本质，促进用户批判性参与而非单纯优化可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM界面过度强化拟人化隐喻，用户常将其视为真人，导致认知偏差和潜在危害。现有界面缺乏替代方案，用户在使用伦理、技术普及和界面选择间存在认知失调。

Method: 将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，构建从透明度驱动的"反拟人化"到诡异"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系。这些隐喻通过引入物质性，揭示LLM作为由人类劳动、基础设施和数据塑造的社会技术系统。

Result: 提出了一种理论框架，展示界面隐喻如何塑造和颠覆默认的拟人化隐喻。该谱系将界面设计重点从优化可用性转向鼓励批判性参与。

Conclusion: 通过将拟人化作为可控设计变量，可以打破默认的拟人化隐喻，帮助用户更准确地理解LLM的本质，减少认知偏差和潜在危害，促进更负责任的技术使用。

Abstract: Anthropomorphizing conversational technology is a natural human tendency. Today, the anthropomorphic metaphor is overly reinforced across intelligent tools. Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly anthropomorphized through interface design. While metaphors are inherently partial, anthropomorphic interfaces highlight similarities between LLMs and humans, but mask crucial differences. As a result, the metaphor is often taken literally; users treat LLMs as if they are truly human. With few safeguards in place, this extreme anthropomorphism drives users to delusion and harm. Users also experience dissonance between the ethics of using LLMs, their growing ubiquity, and limited interface alternatives. We propose repositioning anthropomorphism as a design variable, developing opposing extremes as a theoretical framework for how interface metaphors shape and can disrupt the default metaphor. We introduce a spectrum of metaphors from transparency-driven ''anti-anthropomorphism'' to uncanny ''hyper-anthropomorphism''. These metaphors introduce materiality to interface metaphors, exposing LLMs as sociotechnical systems shaped by human labor, infrastructure, and data. This spectrum shifts interface design away from optimizing usability and toward encouraging critical engagement.

</details>


### [273] [Gamified Informed Decision-Making for Performance-Aware Design by Non-Experts: An Exoskeleton Design Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04643)
*Arman Khalilbeigi Khameneh,Armin Mostafavi,Alicia Nahmad Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个集成游戏引擎与实时性能反馈的决策支持系统，用于帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间，并通过外骨骼立面改造案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持系统在帮助非专业设计师探索复杂性能驱动设计空间方面至关重要。传统设计工具往往缺乏实时反馈和直观交互，限制了非专业人士参与设计决策的能力。

Method: 开发了一个游戏化的决策制定框架，将游戏引擎与实时性能反馈集成。性能标准包括结构行为、环境参数、制造、材料和成本考虑。通过外骨骼立面改造设计案例，对24名建筑学生和非专业设计师进行测试，让他们在实时反馈下迭代修改立面几何形状。

Result: 参与者能够成功迭代修改立面几何形状，并获得三个关键标准的实时反馈：1)结构行为（挠度、质量、应力/强度比）；2)环境参数（太阳得热、供暖/制冷能耗）；3)制造考虑（制造和材料成本、机器人加工、材料设置）。游戏化反馈机制显著增强了用户理解和知情决策能力。

Conclusion: 游戏化环境通过知情决策和性能标准的定制化协商，使利益相关者能够参与设计过程。精心策划的设计空间和结构化指导相比开放式生成工具提高了效率，为入住前评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: Decision Support Systems (DSS) play a crucial role in enabling non-expert designers to explore complex, performance-driven design spaces. This paper presents a gamified decision-making framework that integrates game engines with real-time performance feedback. Performance criteria include structural behavior, environmental parameters, fabrication, material, and cost considerations. The developed design framework was tested with architecture students and non-expert designers on the design of an exoskeleton facade to retrofit an existing building. Participants (N=24) were able to iteratively modify façade geometries while receiving real-time feedback across the three key criteria: 1) structural behavior, including deflection, mass, and stress/strength ratio; 2) environmental parameters, such as solar gain and heating/cooling energy demands; and 3) fabrication considerations, including fabrication and material costs, robotic machining, and material setup. The evaluation of participant interactions reveals that gamified feedback mechanisms significantly enhance user comprehension and informed decision-making across the criteria. Further, participants' understanding of structural, material, and fabrication performance in relation to the iterative design task suggests that curated design spaces and structured guidance improve efficiency compared to open-ended generative tools. This research contributes to pre-occupancy evaluations, demonstrating how gamified environments enable stakeholder participation in the design process through informed decisionmaking and customized negotiation of performance criteria. .

</details>


### [274] [VizCrit: Exploring Strategies for Displaying Computational Feedback in a Visual Design Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04754)
*Mingyi Li,Mengyi Chen,Sarah Luo,Yining Cao,Haijun Xia,Maitraye Das,Steven P. Dow,Jane L. E*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizCrit系统通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成，为设计新手提供可操作性反馈，研究发现解决方案导向反馈比教科书式反馈更能减少设计问题并提升自我感知创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计教学中，教师常提供混合标注和文本的多模态反馈。现有理论强调可操作性反馈的重要性，但如何通过创意工具实现支持这种反馈的标注系统，以及反馈可操作性如何影响新手的设计过程、创造力感知、设计原则学习和最终成果，是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 开发了VizCrit系统，通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成实现计算反馈。采用组间设计实验(N=36)，新手在三种条件下修订设计：教科书式反馈、意识导向反馈、解决方案导向反馈，比较不同反馈类型对设计过程的影响。

Result: 解决方案导向反馈比教科书式反馈导致更少的设计问题和更高的自我感知创造力，但专家对创造力的评分没有显著差异。不同反馈类型在生产力、学习和设计意识发展方面存在权衡。

Conclusion: AI创意支持工具需要校准反馈的可操作性，帮助新手在生产力与学习、成长和设计意识发展之间取得平衡。解决方案导向反馈能提升设计质量和自我效能感，但需注意避免抑制创造性探索。

Abstract: Visual design instructors often provide multi-modal feedback, mixing annotations with text. Prior theory emphasizes the importance of actionable feedback, where "actionability" lies on a spectrum--from surfacing relevant design concepts to suggesting concrete fixes. How might creativity tools implement annotations that support such feedback, and how does the actionability of feedback impact novices' process-related behaviors, perceptions of creativity, learning of design principles, and overall outcomes? We introduce VizCrit, a system for providing computational feedback that supports the actionability spectrum, realized through algorithmic issue detection and visual annotation generation. In a between-subjects study (N=36), novices revised a design under one of three conditions: textbook-based, awareness-centered, or solution-centered feedback. We found that solution-centered feedback led to fewer design issues and higher self-perceived creativity compared with textbook-based feedback, although expert ratings on creativity showed no significant differences. We discuss the implications for AI in Creativity Support Tools, including the potential of calibrating feedback actionability to help novices balance productivity with learning, growth, and developing design awareness.

</details>


### [275] [Can LLMs Synthesize Court-Ready Statistical Evidence? Evaluating AI-Assisted Sentencing Bias Analysis for California Racial Justice Act Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04804)
*Aparna Komarla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Redo.io是一个开源平台，通过处理加州监狱记录生成种族偏见统计证据，并探索LLM驱动的解释层将统计结果转化为连贯法律叙事，评估显示AI可作为实时证据生成的描述性助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加州通过了《种族正义法案》(2020)等立法改革，允许被告基于种族差异统计证据挑战定罪，但政策实施滞后于立法意图，导致"第二次机会差距"——数百个重新量刑机会未被识别。需要工具来识别这些机会并生成法庭就绪的种族偏见证据。

Method: 开发Redo.io开源平台，处理通过《加州公共记录法》获取的95,000份监狱记录；生成法庭就绪的种族偏见统计证据；探索LLM驱动的解释层设计，将优势比、相对风险、卡方检验等统计方法结果合成为连贯叙事，并包含置信区间、样本量和数据限制等上下文信息。

Result: 平台能够处理大量监狱记录并生成统计证据；使用LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估LLM性能与统计学家的比较，结果表明AI可以在分析流程中作为实时证据生成的强大描述性助手。

Conclusion: AI可以作为描述性助手，在分析流程中伦理地整合时，能够为实时证据生成提供强大支持，帮助弥合加州重新量刑中的"第二次机会差距"，促进种族正义法案的有效实施。

Abstract: Resentencing in California remains a complex legal challenge despite legislative reforms like the Racial Justice Act (2020), which allows defendants to challenge convictions based on statistical evidence of racial disparities in sentencing and charging. Policy implementation lags behind legislative intent, creating a 'second-chance gap' where hundreds of resentencing opportunities remain unidentified. We present Redo.io, an open-source platform that processes 95,000 prison records acquired under the California Public Records Act (CPRA) and generates court-ready statistical evidence of racial bias in sentencing for prima facie and discovery motions. We explore the design of an LLM-powered interpretive layer that synthesizes results from statistical methods like Odds Ratio, Relative Risk, and Chi-Square Tests into cohesive narratives contextualized with confidence intervals, sample sizes, and data limitations. Our evaluations comparing LLM performance to statisticians using the LLM-as-a-Judge framework suggest that AI can serve as a powerful descriptive assistant for real-time evidence generation when ethically incorporated in the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [276] [SparkTales: Facilitating Cross-Language Collaborative Storytelling through Coordinator-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04806)
*Wenxin Zhao,Peng Zhang,Hansu Gu,Haoxuan Zhou,Xiaojie Huo,Lin Wang,Wen Zheng,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SparkTales是一个智能支持系统，旨在减轻跨语言协作讲故事活动中协调者的认知负担，同时提升儿童的参与深度和互动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言协作讲故事对儿童语言学习和文化发展至关重要，但实践中儿童参与度较浅，协调者面临认知和组织负担过重的问题，需要协调语言支持、维持儿童参与度并处理文化差异。

Method: 首先进行形成性研究了解协调者的需求和痛点，然后设计SparkTales智能支持系统，该系统利用参与儿童的个体和共同特征，为协调者提供故事框架、多样化问题和理解导向的材料。

Result: 评估结果显示，SparkTales显著提高了协调者的指导效率和质量，同时改善了儿童的参与度，为未来支持跨语言协作的智能系统设计提供了宝贵见解。

Conclusion: SparkTales通过智能支持系统有效解决了跨语言协作讲故事中的协调者负担和儿童参与度问题，为类似场景的智能系统设计提供了成功范例。

Abstract: Cross-language collaborative storytelling plays a vital role in children's language learning and cultural development, fostering both expressive ability and intercultural awareness. Yet, in practice, children's participation is often shallow, and facilitating such sessions places heavy cognitive and organizational burdens on coordinators, who must coordinate language support, maintain children's engagement, and navigate cultural differences. To address these challenges, we conducted a formative study with coordinators to identify their needs and pain points, which guided the design of SparkTales, an intelligent support system for cross-language collaborative storytelling. SparkTales leverages both individual and common characteristics of participating children to provide coordinators with story frameworks, diverse questions, and comprehension-oriented materials, aiming to reduce coordinators' workload while enhancing children's interactive engagement. Evaluation results show that SparkTales not only significantly increases coordinators' efficiency and quality of guidance but also improves children's participation, providing valuable insights for the design of future intelligent systems supporting cross-language collaboration.

</details>


### [277] [Roomify: Spatially-Grounded Style Transformation for Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04917)
*Xueyang Wang,Qinxuan Cen,Weitao Bi,Yunxiang Ma,Xin Yi,Robert Xiao,Xinyi Fu,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Roomify是一个空间锚定的转换系统，能在保持物理房间空间结构和功能语义的同时，生成主题化的虚拟环境，解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR方法面临基本权衡：完全沉浸会牺牲空间感知，而透视解决方案会破坏临场感。需要一种既能保持空间意识又能提供沉浸体验的方法。

Method: 采用空间锚定转换方法，将物理空间视为"空间容器"，保持家具的关键功能和几何属性，同时实现风格上的根本改变。结合现场3D场景理解、AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，创建基于物理现实的个性化虚拟环境。引入跨现实创作工具，通过MR编辑和VR预览工作流实现细粒度用户控制。

Result: 用户研究显示：18名VR用户的临场感比透视方案提高63%，比完全虚拟基线提高26%，同时保持空间感知。8名设计专业人员确认系统的创意表现力（场景质量：5.95/7；创意支持：6.08/7）和跨不同环境的专业工作流价值。

Conclusion: Roomify通过空间锚定转换成功解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题，在保持物理空间结构的同时实现了风格化虚拟环境的生成，为跨现实体验提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Roomify, a spatially-grounded transformation system that generates themed virtual environments anchored to users' physical rooms while maintaining spatial structure and functional semantics. Current VR approaches face a fundamental trade-off: full immersion sacrifices spatial awareness, while passthrough solutions break presence. Roomify addresses this through spatially-grounded transformation - treating physical spaces as "spatial containers" that preserve key functional and geometric properties of furniture while enabling radical stylistic changes. Our pipeline combines in-situ 3D scene understanding, AI-driven spatial reasoning, and style-aware generation to create personalized virtual environments grounded in physical reality. We introduce a cross-reality authoring tool enabling fine-grained user control through MR editing and VR preview workflows. Two user studies validate our approach: one with 18 VR users demonstrates a 63% improvement in presence over passthrough and 26% over fully virtual baselines while maintaining spatial awareness; another with 8 design professionals confirms the system's creative expressiveness (scene quality: 5.95/7; creativity support: 6.08/7) and professional workflow value across diverse environments.

</details>


### [278] [Mind the Gap: Mapping Wearer-Bystander Privacy Tensions and Context-Adaptive Pathways for Camera Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04930)
*Xueyang Wang,Kewen Peng,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统性的多利益相关方评估，揭示了相机眼镜在隐私保护机制上的结构性矛盾，提出了基于情境的动态调整方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机眼镜在佩戴者（寻求记录功能）和旁观者（担心未经授权的监控）之间造成了根本性的隐私紧张关系，需要系统评估隐私机制以解决这一矛盾。

Method: 采用中国样本的问卷调查（N=525）和配对访谈（N=20）进行多利益相关方评估。研究1量化期望-意愿差距，研究2评估12种隐私增强技术并识别基本权衡。

Result: 研究发现：1）旁观者对信息透明度和保护措施的要求始终高于佩戴者愿意提供的水平，敏感情境下65-90%的旁观者会采取防御行动；2）识别出四种基本权衡：可见性vs干扰、赋权vs负担、保护vs自主权、问责vs暴露；3）情境是隐私可接受性的主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 隐私差距反映了结构性不相容而非善意不足。提出情境自适应路径：公共场所最小摩擦可见性、半公共环境结构化协商、敏感情境自动保护。贡献了隐私机制评估的诊断框架和情境感知设计的启示。

Abstract: Camera glasses create fundamental privacy tensions between wearers seeking recording functionality and bystanders concerned about unauthorized surveillance. We present a systematic multi-stakeholder evaluation of privacy mechanisms through surveys (N=525) and paired interviews (N=20) in China. Study 1 quantifies expectation-willingness gaps: bystanders consistently demand stronger information transparency and protective measures than wearers will provide, with disparities intensifying in sensitive contexts where 65-90% of bystanders would take defensive action. Study 2 evaluates twelve privacy-enhancing technologies, revealing four fundamental trade-offs that undermine current approaches: visibility versus disruption, empowerment versus burden, protection versus agency, and accountability versus exposure. These gaps reflect structural incompatibilities rather than inadequate goodwill, with context emerging as the primary determinant of privacy acceptability. We propose context-adaptive pathways that dynamically adjust protection strategies: minimal-friction visibility in public spaces, structured negotiation in semi-public environments, and automatic protection in sensitive contexts. Our findings contribute a diagnostic framework for evaluating privacy mechanisms and implications for context-aware design in ubiquitous sensing.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Advocacy: A Design Space for Replication-Related Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04959)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于复制实验设计的四维度三层次设计空间框架，支持复制研究的决策制定、分类、比较和分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究在科学领域（包括可视化和人机交互）中很重要，但在进行复制时需要决定哪些方面保持不变、哪些需要改变，需要系统化的决策支持框架

Method: 将复制实验设计视为成对比较问题，提出包含四个实践维度的设计空间框架，每个维度由三个比较层次定义，支持回顾性表征和前瞻性规划

Result: 开发了一个多维设计空间框架，可用于识别、分类、比较和分析复制研究的设计决策，提供了实际案例，并与其他复制研究范围描述方法进行了关联

Conclusion: 该框架为复制实验设计提供了系统化的决策支持工具，有助于提高复制研究的透明度和可比性，促进科学领域的复制实践

Abstract: The importance of replication is often discussed and advocated -- not only in the domains of visualization and HCI, but in all scientific areas. When replicating a study, design decisions need to be made with regards which aspects of the original study will remain the same and which will be altered. We present a supporting multi-dimensional design space framework within which such decisions can be identified, categorized, compared and analyzed. The framework treats replication experimental design as a pairwise comparison problem, and represents the design by four practical dimensions defined by three comparison levels. The design space is therefore a framework that can be used for both retrospective characterization and prospective planning. We provide worked examples, and relate our framework to other attempts at describing the scope of replication studies.

</details>


### [280] [Auto-Generating Personas from User Reviews in VR App Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04985)
*Yi Wang,Kexin Cheng,Xiao Liu,Chetan Arora,John Grundy,Thuong Hoang,Henry Been-Lirn Duh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个VR课程中的自动生成人物角色系统，用于促进无障碍需求讨论和指导VR设计开发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物角色是讨论软件设计中无障碍需求的有价值工具，但在VR项目中用于无障碍需求获取的应用仍然有限且面临挑战

Method: 在VR课程中开发了自动生成人物角色系统，使用这些角色来促进无障碍需求讨论并指导VR设计和开发

Result: 自动生成人物角色系统使学生能够更有效地培养同理心，该系统在VR课程环境中能够引出潜在的无障碍需求

Conclusion: 研究表明自动生成的人物角色可以作为在VR课程环境中引出潜在无障碍需求的有效手段

Abstract: Personas are a valuable tool for discussing accessibility requirements in software design and development practices. However, the use of personas for accessibility-focused requirements elicitation in VR projects remains limited and is accompanied by several challenges. To fill this gap, we developed an auto-generated persona system in a VR course, where the personas were used to facilitate discussions on accessibility requirements and to guide VR design and development. Our findings indicate that the auto-generated persona system enabled students to develop empathy more efficiently. This study demonstrates the use of automatically generated personas in VR course settings as a means of eliciting latent accessibility requirements.

</details>


### [281] [Haptics in Cognition: Disruptor or Enabler of Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05019)
*Bibeg Limbu,Irene-Angelica Chounta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性研究显示：增加书写压力（动觉强度）可能轻微损害即时回忆，而单纯触觉操纵（戴手套）无明显影响；心理努力和感知工作量未起中介作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在探索触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）对学习的影响，具体通过手写过程中的信息保留来操作化。研究动机源于理解具身交互与认知结果之间的复杂关系，为设计基于传感器的多模态学习环境提供理论依据。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计的探索性试点研究，N=20名参与者随机分配到四个实验组。操纵两个自变量：触觉（通过戴手套）和动觉强度（通过增加书写压力）。因变量为信息保留（即时回忆测试）。中介变量包括心理努力（次要任务反应时间）和感知工作量（NASA-TLX）。使用贝叶斯二项回归和贝叶斯中介分析进行数据分析。

Result: 贝叶斯二项回归显示中等证据表明增加书写压力对回忆有负面影响（85-88%概率为负效应），而单纯使用手套无明显影响。贝叶斯中介分析未发现心理努力或感知工作量对这些效应有强中介作用的证据，所有95%可信区间包含零，表明存在实质性不确定性。

Conclusion: 增加动觉需求可能轻微损害即时回忆，且这种影响独立于感知工作量或心理努力。单纯触觉操纵似乎不影响信息保留。研究为理解具身交互与认知结果之间的微妙关系做出了贡献，对设计传感器多模态学习环境具有启示意义。

Abstract: This exploratory pilot study investigates the impact of haptic perception --specifically tactile sensitivity (touch) and kinaesthetic intensity (movement)-- on learning, operationalized as information retention (immediate recall) through handwriting. Participants (N=20) were randomly assigned to one of four experimental groups in a 2x2 factorial design, manipulating touch (via glove use) and movement (via increased writing pressure). Information retention was measured using an immediate recall test, while mental effort (reaction time in a secondary task) and perceived workload (NASA-TLX) were examined as mediating variables. Bayesian binomial regression revealed moderate evidence that increased writing pressure negatively influenced recall (85-88% probability of negative effect), whereas glove use alone demonstrated no clear effect. Bayesian mediation analysis found no strong evidence that mental effort or perceived workload mediated these effects, as all 95% credible intervals included zero, indicating substantial uncertainty. These findings suggest that increased Kinaesthetic demands may slightly impair immediate recall, independent of perceived workload or mental effort. Importantly, the manipulation of touch alone does not appear to influence information retention. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between embodied interactions and cognitive outcomes, with implications for designing sensor-based multimodal learning environments.

</details>


### [282] [Wire Your Way: Hardware-Contextualized Guidance and In-situ Tests for Personalized Circuit Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05085)
*Punn Lertjaturaphat,Jungwoo Rhee,Jaewon You,Andrea Bianchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个支持个性化电路构建和调试的原型平台，通过增强型面包板提供电路感知的实时硬件重新配置指导和交互式测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分步教程与创客独特的电路构建偏好和问题调试方式存在不匹配。Arduino等微控制器平台的普及让更多终端用户参与电路原型设计，但现有教程系统无法满足个性化需求。

Method: 开发了一个电路感知的增强型面包板系统，支持实时硬件重新配置的上下文指导，并通过交互式测试进行原位电路验证。系统能够感知电路状态并提供个性化指导。

Result: 通过可用性研究（N=12）表明，创客能够利用电路感知指导和调试功能来支持个性化的构建模式，系统有效解决了传统教程与个性化需求之间的不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 提出的原型平台通过电路感知的增强型面包板和交互式调试功能，成功支持了创客的个性化电路构建和调试需求，弥补了传统分步教程方法的局限性。

Abstract: The increasing popularity of microcontroller platforms like Arduino enables diverse end-user developers to participate in circuit prototyping. Traditionally, follow-along tutorials serve as an essential learning method for makers, and in fact, several prior toolkits leveraged this format as a way to engage new makers. However, literature and our formative study (N=12) show that makers have unique preferences regarding the construction of their circuits and idiosyncratic ways to assess and debug problems, which contrasts with the step-by-step instructional nature of tutorials and those systems leveraging this method. To address this mismatch, we present a prototyping platform that supports personalized circuit construction and debugging. Our system utilizes an augmented breadboard, which is circuit-aware and supports on-the-fly hardware reconfiguration via contextualized guidance and in-situ circuit validation through interactive tests. Through a usability study (N=12), we demonstrate how makers leverage circuit-aware guidance and debugging to support individual building patterns.

</details>


### [283] [Not All Trust is the Same: Effects of Decision Workflow and Explanations in Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05229)
*Laura Spillner,Rachel Ringe,Robert Porzel,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了AI辅助决策中一步与两步工作流设置、解释提供、用户领域知识和AI经验对信任校准的影响，发现两步设置不能减少过度依赖，工作流与解释的交互效应显著，且报告信任与依赖行为是不同的构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助决策中需要实现有根据、良好校准的信任，既要防止过度信任（接受错误的AI建议），也要防止信任不足（拒绝正确的建议）。先前研究在工作流设计（一步vs两步设置）和信任测量（自我报告vs行为信任）上存在差异，需要系统研究这些因素及其交互作用。

Method: 研究考察了(a)决策工作流类型（一步设置：立即看到AI建议；两步设置：先提交初步决策）、(b)解释的存在、(c)用户领域知识和先前AI经验的影响。比较了报告信任、依赖（同意率和切换率）和过度依赖等指标。

Result: 结果显示两步设置不能减少过度依赖。决策工作流对自我报告信任没有直接影响，但与领域知识和解释存在交叉交互效应，表明解释的效果可能无法在不同工作流设置中泛化。研究证实报告信任和依赖行为是不同的构念，应在AI辅助决策中分别评估。

Conclusion: 在AI辅助决策中，两步工作流设置不能有效减少过度依赖，工作流与解释的交互效应需要重视，且必须区分评估报告信任和实际依赖行为这两个不同的构念。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI-assisted decision making is achieving warranted, well-calibrated trust. Both overtrust (accepting incorrect AI recommendations) and undertrust (rejecting correct advice) should be prevented. Prior studies differ in the design of the decision workflow - whether users see the AI suggestion immediately (1-step setup) or have to submit a first decision beforehand (2-step setup) -, and in how trust is measured - through self-reports or as behavioral trust, that is, reliance. We examined the effects and interactions of (a) the type of decision workflow, (b) the presence of explanations, and (c) users' domain knowledge and prior AI experience. We compared reported trust, reliance (agreement rate and switch rate), and overreliance. Results showed no evidence that a 2-step setup reduces overreliance. The decision workflow also did not directly affect self-reported trust, but there was a crossover interaction effect with domain knowledge and explanations, suggesting that the effects of explanations alone may not generalize across workflow setups. Finally, our findings confirm that reported trust and reliance behavior are distinct constructs that should be evaluated separately in AI-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [284] [AttentiveLearn: Personalized Post-Lecture Support for Gaze-Aware Immersive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05324)
*Shi Liu,Martin Feick,Linus Bierhoff,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AttentiveLearn：基于VR眼动追踪的个性化课后测验系统，提升沉浸式学习动机与参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR沉浸式学习环境（如虚拟教室）提供独特学习体验，但有效学习支持仍具挑战。现有HCI研究主要关注课中支持，而课后支持对维持学习动机、参与度和学习成果至关重要，但相关研究不足。

Method: 提出AttentiveLearn学习生态系统，通过VR讲座中的眼动追踪推断学习者注意力分布，在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验。采用为期四周的现场研究，36名大学生参与贝叶斯数据分析讲座，评估系统效果。

Result: AttentiveLearn提高了学习者报告的学习动机和参与度，但学习成效提升的证据不明确。同时，轶事证据表明某些参与者的注意力随时间有所改善。

Conclusion: 基于现场研究结果，为沉浸式学习系统的个性化课后支持提供了实证见解和设计启示，强调个性化课后支持对维持学习动机和参与度的重要性。

Abstract: Immersive learning environments such as virtual classrooms in Virtual Reality (VR) offer learners unique learning experiences, yet providing effective learner support remains a challenge. While prior HCI research has explored in-lecture support for immersive learning, little research has been conducted to provide post-lecture support, despite being critical for sustained motivation, engagement, and learning outcomes. To address this, we present AttentiveLearn, a learning ecosystem that generates personalized quizzes on a mobile learning assistant based on learners' attention distribution inferred using eye-tracking in VR lectures. We evaluated the system in a four-week field study with 36 university students attending lectures on Bayesian data analysis. AttentiveLearn improved learners' reported motivation and engagement, without conclusive evidence of learning gains. Meanwhile, anecdotal evidence suggested improvements in attention for certain participants over time. Based on our findings of the field study, we provide empirical insights and design implications for personalized post-lecture support for immersive learning systems.

</details>
